Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in reflection removal, current methods primarily exploit static image priors from a single frame and still suffer from severe residual artifacts due to the inherent ambiguity between the reflection and transmission layers. In this paper, we propose leveraging event signals to break this ambiguity. By employing event cameras to capture micro-dynamics, we reveal the differential motion between these two layers. We thereby present a novel event-driven reflection removal network, EvReflection, that utilizes these dynamic cues for layer separation. Specifically, we design a Micro-Dynamics Decoupler to disentangle layer-specific motions from event streams as priors, which then guide a Parallax-Attention Rectifier to cleanly remove artifacts from the RGB image. Furthermore, to address data scarcity, we develop a parallax-aware simulation pipeline and construct the EVR$^2$ benchmark dataset, the first real-world dataset for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvReflection achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, surpassing the best competing method by more than 1.6 dB and 1.2 dB in PSNR, respectively. The code, dataset, and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/JiaxiaoWang/EvReflection.

</details>


### [201] [HOPE: Hand-Object Pressure Estimation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06192)
*Subin Jeon,Byungjun Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOPE是一个从单目视频中估计手-物交互过程中接触压力与接触状态的框架，通过将压力估计形式化为以手为中心的逐顶点压力预测问题，实现与物体形状和传感器布局无关的统一输出空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于视觉的压力估计方法仅限于平面表面且仅使用单张图像输入，难以应用于多样物体的动态手-物交互场景，因此需要一种能够适应动态交互、物体形状多样且不依赖特定传感器布局的压力估计方法。

Method: HOPE将压力估计建模为以手为中心的视频预测问题，包含两个关键组件：1) 将触觉手套压力、平面传感器压力和基于距离的接触标注统一映射到手顶点空间，利用裸手接触数据对无度量标签的压力学习进行正则化；2) 引入顶点锚定的视频Transformer，将每个顶点视为持久token，聚合时序上的视觉特征和手部姿态，并使用接触门控压力头确保无接触时压力为零。

Result: 在OpenTouch、PressureVisionDB和手-物接触基准上的实验验证了HOPE在物体压力、表面压力和接触监督的HOI设置中的有效性。尽管度量压力监督主要来自带手套视频，HOPE仍能泛化到裸手自我中心视频和野外视频，产生超越仅接触或平面压力基线的联合接触和压力预测。

Conclusion: HOPE通过手为中心的逐顶点压力预测形式化方法，成功实现了视觉压力估计从平面静态场景到动态多样手-物交互场景的跨越，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Estimating physical pressure from vision is essential for understanding contact-rich hand-object interaction. However, prior vision-based pressure estimation methods are largely limited to planar surfaces and single image input, making them difficult to apply to dynamic hand-object interaction with diverse objects. We instead formulate pressure estimation as a hand-centric video prediction problem with monocular video as input. This formulation predicts temporally evolving per-vertex normal pressure and contact directly on the hand mesh, yielding a unified output space independent of object shape and sensor layout. Building on this formulation, we propose \textbf{HOPE}, a framework with two key components. First, we lift tactile-glove pressure, planar-sensor pressure, and distance-based hand-object contact annotations into a shared hand vertex space, allowing bare-hand contact data to regularize pressure learning where metric labels are unavailable. Second, we introduce a vertex-anchored video transformer that treats each vertex as a persistent token, aggregates visual features and hand pose over time, and uses a contact-gated pressure head to enforce that pressure vanishes without contact. Experiments on OpenTouch, PressureVisionDB, and hand-object contact benchmarks validate HOPE across object-pressure, surface-pressure, and contact-supervised HOI settings. Despite using metric pressure supervision primarily from gloved-hand videos, HOPE generalizes to bare-hand egocentric and in-the-wild videos, producing joint contact and pressure predictions beyond the scope of contact-only or planar-pressure baselines.

</details>


### [202] [CFGPNet: Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network Framework for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06205)
*Nima Hatami,Karim Faez,Saeed Sharifian,Hamidreza Amindavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CFGPNet，一种基于交叉注意力的融合梯度编程网络，用于RGB-T多光谱目标检测。该框架采用改进的GELAN骨干网络、跨模态高效注意力模块（CrossCEA）、注意力选择与聚合融合网络（ASAF）以及可编程梯度辅助分支，在五个多光谱基准数据集上取得了领先性能，并在精度与效率之间实现了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RGB-T目标检测方法存在三个主要问题：(1)跨模态交互不足，未能充分利用可见光与红外图像的互补信息；(2)模态分布差异导致融合不稳定；(3)基于重型注意力的架构计算成本过高。作者旨在提出一种兼顾性能与效率的多光谱目标检测框架。

Method: CFGPNet由四个关键组件构成：(1)改进的GELAN骨干网络，采用RepViT风格的重参数化块以增强特征表示并保持计算效率；(2)Cross Computation Efficient Attention (CrossCEA)模块，增强跨模态特征交互并减少可见光与热红外分支间的冗余信息传递；(3)Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion (ASAF)网络，结合稠密特征聚合与选择性注意力强调，生成紧凑且具判别性的融合表示；(4)可编程梯度辅助分支，集成到每个CFGPNet变体中，以改善梯度传递和优化质量。框架支持三种模型规模。

Result: 在五个公共多光谱基准数据集上的实验结果表明：FLIR上达到80.7% mAP50/45.0% mAP50:95，M3FD上达到89.9%/63.4%，LLVIP上达到97.8%/68.9%，VEDAI上达到83.3%/56.9%，MFAD上达到83.4%/61.8%。这些结果证明CFGPNet在多种场景、目标尺度及模态平衡条件下均表现一致且出色。

Conclusion: CFGPNet是一种有效且实用的多光谱目标检测解决方案，在三种模型规模下均提供了有意义的精度-效率权衡。代码、数据和微调模型已公开于GitHub，便于复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: RGB--T object detection exploits the complementary strengths of visible and infrared imagery, supporting robust perception in low-light, adverse-weather, and complex multi-scale environments. However, existing methods still suffer from insufficient cross-modal interaction, unstable fusion from modality distribution gaps, and the high computational cost of heavy attention-based architectures. To address these issues, CFGPNet is proposed, a Cross-Attention-Based Fused Gradient Programmed Network framework for multispectral object detection. CFGPNet uses an improved GELAN backbone with RepViT-style re-parameterized blocks to strengthen feature representation while preserving computational efficiency. A Cross Computation Efficient Attention (CrossCEA) module is introduced to enhance cross-modal feature interaction and reduce redundant information transfer between visible and thermal branches. To generate compact and discriminative fused representations, an Attention Selection and Aggregation Fusion (ASAF) network combines dense feature aggregation with selective attention-based emphasis. Moreover, a programmable-gradient auxiliary branch is integrated into each CFGPNet variant to improve gradient delivery and optimization quality. Experiments on five public multispectral benchmarks, FLIR, M3FD, LLVIP, VEDAI, and MFAD, demonstrate that CFGPNet achieves strong and consistent performance across diverse scenes, object scales, and modality balances. In particular, the framework attains 80.7% mAP50 / 45.0% mAP50:95 on FLIR, 89.9% / 63.4% on M3FD, and 97.8% / 68.9% on LLVIP. It also reaches 83.3% / 56.9% on VEDAI and 83.4% / 61.8% on MFAD. These results show that CFGPNet is an effective, practical solution offering useful accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across three model scales. The code, data, and fine-tuned models are available at https://github.com/NimaHatami99/CFGPNet.

</details>


### [203] [EmoWorld: A Decoupled Affective Field for Controllable Emotional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06231)
*Bingyuan Wang,Baistan Zhyldyzbekov,Kunyu Feng,Zeyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoWorld是一个在冻结的流匹配视频扩散Transformer（Video DiT）中解耦情感、语义提示与时间进程三种因素的情绪驱动视频生成框架。它通过一次性准备阶段提取层间情感方向与可复用提示库，在推理时分别通过VAS（视觉氛围调控）、SAS（语义情感调控）和TAS（时间情感调控）三个模块注入干预信号，在不更新生成器参数的前提下实现了目标情感对齐与时间平滑性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器将全局氛围、蕴含情感语义的提示信息和时间发展过程全部纠缠在单一文本条件中，无法独立控制这些因素。情感对观众理解场景至关重要，因此需要一种能够解耦这些混杂因素的框架，实现对情感表现力的精细可控。

Method: EmoWorld包含三个核心组件：(1) 一次性准备阶段，从几何保持的中性与情感编辑全景中提取层间情感方向（affect directions）和可复用提示库；(2) Visual Atmosphere Steering（VAS）将情感氛围方向注入隐藏状态；(3) Semantic Affective Steering（SAS）分离出可独立缩放的情感提示残差以控制语义线索；(4) Temporal Affective Steering（TAS）在去噪过程与视频时间维度上对端点残差场进行插值，实现情感的时间演进控制。整体在冻结的Video DiT上运行，不更新生成器参数。

Result: 在Wan2.2上，VAS使目标情感对齐提升19%同时将时间波动代理指标降低48%；SAS使目标情感对齐提升37%且检测到的情感提示线索增加36%；TAS使情感转换单调性相比最强基线提升15%。EmoWorld在27个情感类别下于文生视频和图生视频设置中均进行了评估，并展示了跨多种Video-DiT骨干网络的可移植性，支持相机条件合成。

Conclusion: EmoWorld证明了在冻结的视频扩散Transformer中解耦情感氛围、语义情感提示与时间进程是可行且有效的，无需更新生成器参数即可大幅提升情感对齐与时间一致性，且方法具有良好的跨骨干网络可移植性和相机条件扩展能力。

Abstract: Emotion shapes how viewers interpret a scene, yet existing video generators entangle global atmosphere, affect-bearing semantic cues, and temporal progression within a single text condition. We present EmoWorld, a framework that decouples these factors within a frozen flow-matching video diffusion transformer (Video DiT). A one-time preparation stage extracts layer-specific affect directions and a reusable cue library from geometry-preserving neutral and emotion-edited panoramas. At inference, Visual Atmosphere Steering (VAS) injects atmosphere directions into hidden states, Semantic Affective Steering (SAS) isolates a separately scalable prompt residual for semantic cues, and Temporal Affective Steering (TAS) interpolates endpoint residual fields across denoising and video time. On Wan2.2, VAS improves target-emotion alignment by 19% while reducing a temporal-fluctuation proxy by 48%; SAS improves target-emotion alignment by 37% and increases detected affect-bearing cues by 36%; and TAS improves transition monotonicity by 15% over the strongest baseline. EmoWorld is evaluated across 27 emotion categories in text-to-video and image-to-video settings, demonstrates portability across multiple Video-DiT backbones, and supports camera-conditioned composition without updating generator parameters.

</details>


### [204] [Depth-Guided Video Object Counting in Crowded Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06236)
*Yuanjing Xu,Xinyan Liu,Weidong Chen,Zixuan Zou,Linhao Zhang,Zhuangzhe Meng,Antoni B. Chan,Weigang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度引导检测器（DG-Det），结合多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力和显式遮挡预测，解决拥挤和遮挡场景下的视频目标计数问题，并引入统一的去重框架消除跨帧冗余计数，同时发布了新的RGB-D视频目标计数数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频目标计数方法仅依赖RGB信息，在拥挤和遮挡条件下判别能力受限，无法有效区分目标类别并实现精准计数。

Method: 1）提出DG-Det，通过融合深度线索与多尺度RGB-D交叉注意力增强空间理解；2）引入显式遮挡预测提升拥挤场景下的检测鲁棒性；3）设计统一的去重框架消除跨帧冗余计数；4）发布包含深度信息和多类别目标的RGB-D视频计数数据集。

Result: 在现有基线基础上MAE降低62.01%，RMSE也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 深度信息显著提升视频目标计数在拥挤和遮挡场景下的性能，所提出的方法、后处理流程及数据集为该领域提供了新的基准和资源。

Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [205] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个基于流匹配的无GAN非配对图像翻译框架，通过学习到的逐特征门控替代全局噪声/引导控制，在ODE积分中同时控制初始化和传输时序，实现对"保留什么"和"改变什么"的逐像素区分，在五个自然与生物医学基准上取得最优或接近最优的FID/KID结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的非配对图像翻译方法使用单一的全局噪声或引导值控制图像保留程度，无法在空间上区分需要保留的内容与需要改变的外观。这限制了翻译质量与结构保真度之间的平衡。

Method: PRISM采用无GAN的流匹配框架，学习一个逐特征门控。门控的空间先验来自每个源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离——远离目标的特征被释放，与目标一致的特征被保留。同一门控同时控制初始化（混合真实源潜变量与任务匹配的损坏）和ODE积分过程中的传输时序。损坏方式与任务匹配：内容锚定（AdaIN）用于保结构翻译，部分锚定用于变结构翻译。门控可在推理时由文本或检测器局部覆盖而无需重训。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ猫→狗、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、白昼→夜晚再照明、虚拟染色、乳腺冷冻→永久组织病理）的共享同分割协议下，PRISM在四个基准上取得最优Inception FID和KID，在第五个上结果有竞争力；在组织病理学上核计数比最接近理想值，表明在目标真实感与结构保留之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: PRISM通过逐特征门控将"保留"与"改变"的决策从全局尺度细化到局部特征尺度，显著改善了非配对翻译中的空间可控性，且支持推理时灵活的用户干预，兼顾真实感与结构保真。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [206] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38（10,874张专家验证的孟加拉手语图像，覆盖38种手型/51个孟加拉字母），并设计了一个仅29.8万参数的轻量级注意力卷积网络，从零训练达到96.37%准确率，接近（差1.08个百分点）九种ImageNet预训练模型的最佳表现，但参数少8.5-68倍、计算量少1.3-21.7倍；量化后仅0.48MB，在智能手机上每张图像3.98ms，验证了从基准精度到可部署辅助技术的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋人群体依靠孟加拉手语（BdSL）交流，但现有自动识别系统存在两大问题：一是使用无专家验证的受控环境数据集，缺乏真实场景可靠性；二是依赖重量级预训练骨干网络，参数和计算开销大，不适合在个人设备（如智能手机）上部署。作者希望构建一个既精确又轻量、可在个人设备上运行的BdSL识别系统，以扩大聋人群体对教育和服务的访问。

Method: 本文方法包含两部分：(1) 数据构建——从孟加拉三所特殊教育学校采集真实手语者的图像，并通过专家验证，构建覆盖全部38种手型（对应51个孟加拉字母）的RSBdSL38数据集，共10,874张图像；(2) 模型设计——提出轻量级注意力卷积网络（约298,470参数），由分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双池化和Swish激活组成，从零训练而非使用预训练权重。同时进行了消融实验（移除架构组件导致7.61-89.30点下降，训练配方仅最多3.17点下降）、Grad-CAM可解释性验证、跨域泛化（六个公共基准）、零样本测试和量化部署测试。

Result: 模型从零训练达到96.37%准确率（五种子均值95.72%±0.54%），与九种ImageNet预训练高效架构中最佳者仅差1.08个百分点，但参数减少8.5-68倍、MACs减少1.3-21.7倍。在六个公共BdSL基准上重训后达92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%。架构消融显示每个组件都至关重要（移除任一阶段损失7.61-89.30点）。Grad-CAM确认预测关注于签名手部。保持6/36手语者的签名者独立测试为85.18%。量化至0.48MB后，在商业智能手机15.5MB占用的应用中每张图像仅需3.98ms。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集和从零训练的轻量模型共同证明：无需依赖重型预训练骨干网络，即可在保持与预训练方法相当甚至接近的准确率的同时，大幅降低参数和计算成本，实现真实设备上的BdSL识别部署。这为孟加拉聋人群体提供了切实可用的无障碍技术方案，数据集、代码和模型均已开源发布。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [207] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAS框架，通过显式解耦全局权威共享状态与视图渲染，解决多玩家视频世界模型中的状态纠缠、计算冗余和视图不一致问题，在1024并发玩家、10000步长预测上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型在多玩家环境中将世界状态与视角相关的视觉潜变量纠缠在一起，导致冗余计算、视角不一致和可扩展性差。作者观察到多玩家游戏架构中使用权威共享状态的设计可解决此问题，因此受此启发提出MAS。

Method: MAS受多玩家游戏架构启发，解耦世界动力学与视图渲染。包含两个核心组件：1) 学习型逻辑引擎（Logic Engine），从联合行动推进全局权威类型的共享状态，无需手写状态转移函数，作为唯一的循环记忆和同步参考；2) 学习型渲染引擎（Rendering Engine），从共享状态按需为任意请求的相机生成独立一致的视图。

Result: 在匹配的多玩家Snake基准上，MAS相比最先进的多视角基线实现了更高的状态准确性和更低的跨视角不一致性；能够推进包含1024个并发玩家的预测世界并持续10000个递归步骤。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展、一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [208] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OTLesMix的新型图像合成方法，利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划生成真实且多样化的脑部病灶合成样本。在三个脑病灶分割任务上，该方法相比不使用合成数据训练的模型将Dice评分提升了2.9至6.6个点，并优于现有的基于混合策略的最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于混合策略的数据增强方法在生成病变形状和位置方面存在有限的变异性，无法充分多样化训练数据，从而限制了模型在医学图像分割任务上的性能提升。

Method: OTLesMix是一种新颖的图像合成方法，通过利用Wasserstein重心和最优传输计划来生成真实且多样的样本，以克服传统混合方法在病变形状和位置变异上的局限性。

Result: 在三个脑病变分割任务上的实验表明，OTLesMix相比未使用合成数据训练的基线模型将Dice评分提高了2.9到6.6个点，并超越了现有的基于混合策略的最先进方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix通过最优传输理论驱动的样本合成策略，有效增强了医学图像分割模型的训练效果，验证了其在病灶形状和位置多样性生成方面的优势。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [209] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号模型，利用定制Mask R-CNN管道处理1,272张标注图像，并通过掩膜灰世界白平衡和解剖约束检测层两个领域感知机制来应对患者生成健康数据的变异性。模型在内部测试集和外部数据集上均表现优异，且开源提供，为远程牙科筛查提供了低成本、可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人受口腔健康问题困扰，但专业牙科护理成本高且可及性有限。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口内相机图像，普通公众无法获取这些设备进行自我筛查，亟需一种基于普通智能手机照片的自动化口腔健康筛查方案。

Method: 开发定制的Mask R-CNN管道，在1,272张标注的智能手机图像上训练。管道包含两个领域感知机制：(1)掩膜灰世界白平衡算法，用于减轻人为色偏；(2)解剖约束检测层，用于强制执行结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估分四个阶段：内部留出测试、独立外部测试、描述性消融研究和基于同一内部测试集的折叠式训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上模型达到实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，类别感知PQ为0.780，操作F1为0.884；十次运行的训练稳定性标准偏差仅0.009。外部数据集上AP@50为0.901，PQ为0.832，F1为0.928，尽管外部数据在人群、传感器和采集协议上存在差异。推理管道以开源容器化API形式提供。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像能够支持自动化牙齿级解剖映射，为资源受限环境中的远程筛查和远程牙科提供可扩展、潜在低成本的解决方案，具备公共卫生应用价值。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [210] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc在LightLoc架构基础上扩展了各向异性高斯协方差头，为每个体素预测完整的3x3正定协方差矩阵，并使用NLL损失和kNN空间平滑正则化进行训练。推理阶段采用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，包含不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验。实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上持续提升，同时产生校准良好的协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LiDAR的场景坐标回归方法只产生确定性预测，丢弃了偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），这种不确定性信息本可用于提升鲁棒性和下游决策质量。

Method: 在LightLoc架构上增加一个各向异性高斯协方差头，为每个体素预测完整的3x3正定协方差矩阵；训练使用NLL损失并辅以kNN空间平滑正则化器；推理采用修改后的SC2-PCR求解器，使用不确定性加权种子评分和马氏距离内点检验；采用ECE评估预测不确定性质量。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位准确性上取得一致改进，同时生成良好校准的协方差。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模偶然不确定性，UQ-Loc不仅提升了定位精度，还提供了经过良好校准的不确定性估计，为可靠的下游决策提供了基础。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [211] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了超分辨率重建在白质高信号(WMH)分割前应用的效果，发现其主要效应是抹除小病灶而非产生虚假病灶，且ECLARE方法在恢复小病灶信号上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床FLAIR扫描通常为厚层采集，面内分辨率差，超分辨率(SR)常用于恢复各向同性体积。然而，在WMH分割前应用SR是否保留病灶内容尚不明确——模型可能抹除小的真实病灶或产生不存在的虚假病灶。这是首次系统评估SR前处理对WMH分割的影响。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29例个体的1mm各向同性高分辨率(HR)FLAIR扫描，由专家手动分割WMH，并模拟退化至3mm和5mm层间采集。比较三种重建方法：多对比隐式神经表示(INR)、单对比自监督模型(ECLARE)和三次插值。采用对HR小病灶最敏感的MARS-WMH分割方法进行逐病灶分析，评价指标包括检测灵敏度、抹除率和幻觉率。

Result: SR的主要效应是抹除小的真实病灶而非产生幻觉，且抹除率随层厚增加而增大，但所有重建方法仍优于原始厚层扫描。ECLARE在两种层厚下对小病灶信号恢复最佳，而INR并不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: 在WMH分割前应用SR会以牺牲小病灶为代价，选择合适的方法（如ECLARE）可以减轻这种抹除效应；隐式神经表示方法在此任务上并无优势。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Post-Hoc Trajectory-Risk Certification for Modular LLM-Based Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05199)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究自主安全代理多阶段流水线（如网络流量分类→攻击技术归因）的轨迹级分布自由保证问题。证明自然的三阶段以上成对相关性扩展无效（给出下界而非上界），提出有效的生成树替代方案，区分阶段依赖性与审计样本量是否足以认证该依赖性，并给出匹配的上下样本复杂度界。实验表明粗到细标签选择会制造虚假相关性，去除伪影后测量相关性从近1降至0-0.78；直接审计比Bonferroni紧13.7%（样本充足时），模块化证书平均增益0.6%。12个配置下平均轨迹覆盖率92.7%±2.4%（alpha=0.10）。跨数据集部署时单步误覆盖率达100%，即使准确率仍为78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主安全代理以多阶段流水线形式部署（如先分类网络流量再归因攻击技术），每阶段可用分裂共形预测获得有限样本覆盖保证，但部署需要整条链路的轨迹级保证。由于各阶段独立训练和校准，这些保证不能自动组合。Bonferroni分配虽然分布无关但在误差相关时保守，需要更有效的组合保证方法。

Method: 提出生成树替代方案替代无效的成对相关性扩展；区分阶段依赖性与审计样本量对依赖性的认证能力，推导匹配的上下样本复杂度界；设计去除粗到细标签选择伪影的方法；构建模块化证书（使用每阶段证书加成对重叠界）；通过同模型、跨模型和置换配对检验来区分共享样本难度与共享模型表示。

Result: 粗到细标签选择可制造近完美的虚假相关性，去除伪影后相关性从近1降至0-0.78（6个开放LLM、2个数据集的两阶段入侵检测流水线）。直接审计在样本量达标时比Bonferroni紧13.7%，样本不足时更差。模块化证书平均增益0.6%。平均轨迹覆盖率92.7%±2.4%（alpha=0.10）。跨数据集部署时单步误覆盖率达100%而准确率仍为78%。

Conclusion: 多阶段安全流水线的轨迹级保证不能通过简单的成对相关性扩展实现，需采用有效的生成树方法。粗到细标签选择引入的测量伪影会严重高估阶段间相关性；残余依赖主要反映共享样本难度而非共享模型表示。跨数据集分布偏移会先破坏校准置信度再影响原始准确率，凸显分布偏移对安全性保证的严重威胁。

Abstract: Autonomous security agents operate as staged pipelines, such as classifying network traffic and then attributing attacks to a specific technique. Split conformal prediction gives each stage finite-sample coverage, but deployment requires a trajectory-level guarantee across the full chain. These guarantees do not compose automatically when stages are independently trained and calibrated. Bonferroni allocation is distribution-free but conservative under correlated errors. We show that a natural pairwise-correlation extension to three or more stages is invalid because it gives a lower rather than an upper bound, and derive a valid spanning-tree alternative. We distinguish whether stages are dependent from whether an audit sample is large enough to certify that dependence, and give matching upper and information-theoretic lower sample-complexity bounds. We also show that coarse-to-fine label selection can create near-perfect measured correlation without learned dependence.
  On a two-stage intrusion-detection pipeline across 6 open LLMs and 2 datasets, removing this artifact reduces measured correlation from near 1 to 0-0.78. A direct audit of trajectory failure becomes 13.7% tighter than Bonferroni once the audit reaches the required sample size, but is worse when undersized. A modular certificate using per-stage certificates and a pairwise overlap bound yields a positive average gain of 0.6%, quantifying the cost of lacking joint access. Same-model, cross-model, and permuted-pairing tests show that residual dependence reflects shared sample difficulty, not shared model representations. Average trajectory coverage across 12 configurations is 92.7% +/- 2.4% at alpha = 0.10. Under cross-dataset deployment, single-step miscoverage reaches 100% even when accuracy remains 78%, showing that distribution shift destroys calibrated confidence before raw accuracy.

</details>


### [213] [ASTELD: A Six-Axis Classification Framework for Autonomous AI Agents - Design, Evaluation, and an OpenClaw Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05201)
*Siyuan Li,Peng Shu,Churan Yu,Peilong Wang,Ruidong Zhang,Bowen Guo,Xinliang Li,Ruiyu Yan,Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Wei Ruan,Lifeng Chen,Junhao Chen,Zhaojun Ding,Yiwei Li,Zhengliang Liu,Haixing Dai,Lin Zhao,Yu Bao,Xiang Li,Wei Zhang,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASTELD，一个用于自主AI代理平台分类的六轴操作框架（架构模式、安全态势、工具集成模型、执行范式、自主性与人工控制水平、部署拓扑），并通过映射八个代表性框架和OpenClaw深度案例研究验证其区分力与解释力，揭示跨平台模式和空置设计区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理平台在架构、安全、工具集成、执行、自主性和部署等方面差异巨大，但领域内缺乏统一的分类方案来比较这些设计选择，亟需一个可复现的分类框架以支持平台比较、设计空间探索和框架选型。

Method: ASTELD框架通过综合先前的代理分类学、可观察的平台属性和明确的类别分配规则构建而成；采用八个代表性框架进行判别力和解释性效用评估，并以OpenClaw作为深入案例研究，进一步对50余个OpenClaw衍生品进行分类分析。

Result: ASTELD成功区分了所有八个平台的主要配置，揭示了三个跨平台模式（安全-可访问性对角线、强执行-架构耦合、能力趋同但架构持续分化）；对OpenClaw衍生物的分类显示创新集中在安全、执行和部署三个轴；案例研究还提供了六类漏洞分类学、五个机构评估证据以及采用与治理分析。同时发现一个重要的空置区域：没有任何被评估系统同时具备本地优先部署与企业级安全。

Conclusion: ASTELD可作为比较代理平台、识别未占设计区域、指导框架选择和组织未来实证研究的可复现方法；同时暴露了本地优先部署与企业级安全结合这一未开发的空白设计区域。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agent platforms differ substantially in architecture, security, tool integration, execution, autonomy, and deployment, yet the field lacks a common classification scheme for comparing these design choices. We propose ASTELD, an operational six-axis classification framework for autonomous AI agents: Architecture pattern, Security posture, Tool integration model, Execution paradigm, Level of autonomy and human control, and Deployment topology. ASTELD is constructed by synthesizing prior agent taxonomies with observable platform properties and explicit category-assignment rules. We evaluate its discriminative and explanatory utility by mapping eight representative frameworks and by using OpenClaw as an in-depth case study. The resulting profiles separate all eight platforms under their dominant configurations and reveal three cross-platform patterns: a security-accessibility diagonal, strong execution-architecture coupling, and capability convergence with persistent architectural differentiation. We further classify 50+ OpenClaw derivatives and find that innovation concentrates on the Security, Execution, and Deployment axes, indicating that ASTELD can explain where ecosystem fragmentation occurs. The OpenClaw case study also supplies a six-category vulnerability taxonomy, evidence from five institutional assessments, and adoption and governance analyses that connect platform coordinates to observed risks. These results position ASTELD as a reproducible method for comparing agent platforms, identifying unoccupied design regions, guiding framework selection, and organizing future empirical research. The analysis also exposes a consequential empty region: none of the evaluated systems combines local-first deployment with enterprise-grade security.

</details>


### [214] [A Survey of Adversarial Efficiency Degradation for Vision Transformer by Exploiting Input-adaptive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05217)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了针对视觉Transformer（ViTs）输入自适应推理机制的效率降级攻击（如SlowFormer和DeSparsify），分析了这类攻击如何在不降低模型精度的前提下增加计算开销，并评估了现有防御手段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViTs越来越多地依赖输入自适应推理（如token剪枝和提前停止）来满足能耗和延迟预算，但这些机制容易被恶意攻击利用来增加计算量。为设计轻量级防御措施以保护低功耗部署场景（如移动/嵌入式设备），需要系统化理解这类效率降级攻击的原理和影响。

Method: 作者统一并比较了两种代表性攻击方法——SlowFormer（通用对抗性补丁）和DeSparsify（逐图像扰动），在三种流行的token剪枝框架（A-ViT、ATS、AdaViT）上进行了标准化评估，采用GFLOPs、精度损失和Attack Success（AS）指标量化攻击效果，并围绕三个核心问题展开分析：输入自适应优化如何产生攻击面、攻击如何运作及哪些优化最脆弱、现有防御手段能否有效恢复效率。

Result: 文章通过统一的评估指标（GFLOPs、精度损失、AS）对比了不同攻击策略在不同剪枝框架下的表现，识别出最脆弱的优化机制，并评估了现有防御方法在攻击下恢复计算效率的实际效果，发现部分防御措施虽然能缓解风险但可能引入额外开销。

Conclusion: 效率降级攻击是ViTs面临的新型安全威胁，理解其攻击面和防御局限性对于设计轻量级、适用于低功耗环境的防护机制至关重要；未来需要开发既能有效缓解计算膨胀攻击又不显著增加推理开销的防御策略。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) increasingly rely on input-adaptive inference, such as token pruning and early halting, to meet energy and latency budgets. This survey examines a recent class of adversarial efficiency degradation attacks that target these mechanisms to increase computation without necessarily degrading accuracy. We unify and compare two representative attacks, SlowFormer (a universal adversarial patch) and DeSparsify (per-image perturbations), across three popular token-pruning frameworks: A-ViT, ATS, and AdaViT. We standardize reporting using GFLOPs, accuracy loss, and an Attack Success (AS) metric that measures how much of the model's compute savings the attack takes away. Understanding these attacks is crucial for designing countermeasures that not only mitigate risk but also remain lightweight, since deployment often occurs in low-power settings such as mobile or embedded devices. To organize our analysis, we focus on three questions: how input-adaptive optimizations (e.g., token pruning and early halting) create attack surfaces for efficiency degradation; how such attacks operate in practice and which optimizations are most vulnerable; and which defenses exist today and whether they meaningfully restore efficiency under attack.

</details>


### [215] [CyberBridge: Bridging the Gap Between Cybersecurity Education and Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05231)
*Arthur Nijdam,Paul Stankovski Wagner,Sara Ramezanian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberBridge是一个将网络安全职位描述自动映射到专业角色画像的框架。它通过将职位描述分解为知识、技能和任务（KST）陈述，使用sentence-BERT模型进行嵌入，并与最语义相似的角色画像匹配。其核心贡献在于可解释性——用户可追溯每条推荐背后的具体能力依据。该框架支持岗位推荐、市场分析和课程规划三个主要用例，可作为教育机构进行职业指导和循证课程开发的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全领域快速演变，学术项目难以将课程与行业实践中预期的能力要求对齐，毕业生能力与市场需求之间存在显著缺口。

Method: 将职位描述分解为知识、技能和任务（KST）陈述，使用sentence-BERT模型对KST语句进行语义嵌入，并匹配最相似的专业角色画像；通过保留每个匹配背后的具体能力依据，实现推荐的可解释性。

Result: 该框架支持三个核心用例：面向学生的岗位推荐、面向教育者和课程开发者的市场分析，以及面向在需求岗位准备的课程规划评估。

Conclusion: CyberBridge可作为教育机构在网络安全教育中进行职业指导和基于证据的课程开发的实用工具，弥合学术培养与行业需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This full research paper presents CyberBridge, a framework that automatically maps cybersecurity job descrip- tions to professional role profiles. As the cybersecurity landscape evolves rapidly, it is difficult for academic programs to align curricula with the competencies expected in practice.
  CyberBridge addresses this gap by decomposing a given vacancy description into its constituent Knowledge, Skill, and Task (KST) statements, embedding them using a sentence- BERT model, and matching them to the most semantically similar workforce profiles. A key contribution of our approach is its interpretability. Rather than functioning as a black box, CyberBridge enables users to trace recommendations back to the specific competencies driving each match, providing a human- readable justification for the resulting mappings.
  CyberBridge supports three primary use cases; (1) job recom- mendation, providing students with vacancies and closely match- ing professional roles based on their completed curriculum, (2) market analysis, enabling educators and curriculum developers to analyze which roles are currently in demand, and (3) curriculum planning, assessing which course program best prepares students for in-demand cybersecurity roles.
  As such, CyberBridge can be used by educational institutions as a practical tool for career guidance and evidence-based curriculum development in cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [216] [The Hidden Life of Public Safety Communications Signals: A Comparative Security Analysis of TETRA, TETRAPOL, and P25](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05247)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL等集群陆地移动无线电（LMR）系统中信令平面的重大安全缺陷。即使内容加密完美，被动接收的软件定义无线电（SDR）观察者仍可从暴露的信令中推断出网络拓扑、单元位置、单位存在性、跨小区和分组移动性、组织结构和密钥轮换等操作敏感信息，实现从本地嗅探到全国网络映射的能力升级，并展示了TETRAPOL中如何利用信令推断和紧急呼叫处理的保密缺陷实现未加密语音提取，最后提出了针对性缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共安全机构和关键基础设施运营商依赖基于P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL的集群LMR系统，这些系统被期望不仅保护通信内容，还要保护通信事实本身。然而，现有安全分析主要集中在内容平面的安全性（如恢复加密密钥或捕获意外明文），缺乏对信令平面暴露所导致的对抗性推断威胁的系统性评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示即使内容加密完美，被动信令观察仍能泄露相当敏感的信息。

Method: 作者采用被动、仅接收的软件定义无线电（SDR）作为观察者，对P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL三种集群LMR标准进行信令平面推断攻击分析与实证。研究通过被动嗅探信令元数据，利用时序和关联分析来推测网络拓扑与地理细节、单元存在性、跨小区和分组的移动性、组织结构以及密钥领域和密钥纪元轮换等操作安全细节，并特别针对TETRAPOL演示了信令推断与紧急呼叫处理的标准级保密失败相结合的语音提取攻击路径。

Result: 研究表明，即使在内容加密完美的假设下，被动信令观察者仍能恢复操作敏感的网络拓扑和地理信息、单元存在性、跨小区/分组的移动模式、组织结构和特殊密钥域及密钥纪元轮换等安全细节。更严重的是，信令平面推断能力可扩展至观察者直接接收范围之外，将本地嗅探转变为全国范围的网络映射能力，从而削弱或击败LMR标准的身份混淆机制。对TETRAPOL，作者演示了信令元数据推断与标准级保密性失败相结合可实现未加密语音的提取。

Conclusion: LMR标准在信令平面与内容平面之间存在着显著的安全缺口，这是一个系统性、跨标准的安全问题。作者建议采取针对性的保护措施，包括保护跨小区、基站和用户身份的标识符，以缩小信令暴露所带来的推断攻击面，从而提升整个集群LMR生态系统的安全性和隐私保护水平。

Abstract: Public-safety agencies and critical infrastructure operators rely on trunked land-mobile radio (LMR) systems, based on P25, TETRA, and TETRAPOL. These systems are expected to protect not just the content of a communication but the fact of it. Yet LMR standards leave a stark gap between confidentiality of \emph{content} and of \emph{communication}: underneath an encrypted traffic plane, their signaling plane is almost entirely in the clear. We probe the depth and impact of adversarial inference from this exposed signaling.
  Prior security analyses of these systems have concentrated on the content plane---recovering encryption keys or capturing accidental cleartext. We show that comparably sensitive information can be \emph{inferred from passively observed signaling even if the content encryption were perfect}.
  In particular, we show that across the trunked LMR standards, a passive, receive-only software-defined radio (SDR) observer can recover operationally sensitive network topology and geography details, unit presence, mobility across cells and groups, organizational structure, as well as operational security details such as special key domains and key-epoch rotation. This signaling-plane inference reaches far beyond the observer's direct area of reception, turning \emph{local} sniffing into \emph{nationwide} network mapping capabilities that degrade or defeat LMR standards' identity obfuscation through timing and association. In the case of TETRAPOL, we demonstrate how inference and tracking of such signaling metadata and a standards-level confidentiality failure in emergency call handling enable unencrypted voice extraction.
  Finally, we discuss potential countermeasures and mitigations, including specific recommendations for protecting inter-cell, base station and subscriber identities.

</details>


### [217] [eMicro: Real-Time Multi-Hop Access Control for Microservices with eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05300)
*Rizky Ramadhana Putra,Osama Bajaber,Saimon Amanuel Tsegai,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo,Yuede Ji,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: eMicro是一个面向微服务架构的路径感知防御系统，通过基于历史的访问控制扩展、确定性有限自动机（DFA）编码安全策略以及eBPF内核态请求追踪三项核心技术，实现实时多跳访问控制，有效防御多跳攻击。实验表明其可在1微秒内完成策略检查，仅用100MB存储5000万条策略，并将传播开销降低90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代云应用由数千个微服务组成，服务间交互形成复杂的请求路径。传统的服务间访问控制只限制单个服务到服务的请求，无法防御多跳攻击——即每一跳看似合法但整体路径违反安全意图的攻击，导致系统面临未授权访问和数据泄露的风险。

Method: eMicro通过三项关键技术实现实时多跳访问控制：(1) 将基于历史的访问控制扩展以捕获服务调用序列；(2) 将安全策略编码为高效的确定性有限自动机（DFA），支持常数时间查找和紧凑的标签传播；(3) 使用eBPF实现内核态请求追踪，实现透明、低开销的强制执行，无需修改代码。

Result: 在DeathStarBench以及Uber、阿里巴巴和字节跳动的生产云追踪数据上进行了评估，覆盖1200万个请求工作流和数千个服务。eMicro的策略检查耗时仅1微秒，5000万条策略仅需100MB存储空间，传播开销降低90%，运行时影响可忽略不计，展现出了良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: eMicro在大型微服务环境中提供了可扩展且高效的防护能力，能够有效抵御多跳攻击，具备在大规模微服务环境中实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Modern cloud applications often comprise thousands of microservices whose interactions form complex request paths. Traditional inter-service access control restricts individual service-to-service requests, but fails to prevent multi-hop attacks, where each hop appears legitimate yet the overall path violates security intent. This gap leaves systems exposed to unauthorized access and data exfiltration. In this paper, we present eMicro, a path-aware defense system for microservices that prevents such attacks while remaining efficient and deployable. eMicro enforces real-time multi-hop access control through three key techniques: (1) history-based access control extended to capture service invocation sequences; (2) security policies encoded as efficient deterministic finite automaton (DFA), supporting constant-time lookups and compact label propagation; (3) eBPF-based in-kernel request tracing for transparent, low-overhead enforcement without code changes. Evaluations on DeathStarBench and production cloud traces from Uber, Alibaba, and ByteDance, covering 12 million request workflows and thousands of services, demonstrate the scalability of eMicro. eMicro performs policy checks in 1 microsecond, stores 50 million policies in only 100 MB, and reduces propagation overhead by 90% with negligible runtime impact. These results show that eMicro delivers scalable and efficient protection against multi-hop attacks, making it practical for deployment in large-scale microservice environments.

</details>


### [218] [The Trust-Free Aggregation Layer of the Unicity Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05316)
*Risto Laanoja,Mike Gault,Dirk Draheim,Ahto Buldas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Unicity，一种新型区块链基础设施，支持用户执行链下点对点代币交易，同时通过聚合层防止代币双重支付。聚合层采用仅追加的键值存储，每轮使用Radix稀疏Merkle树（RSMT）生成加密摘要，并向共识层提供追加一致性的密码学证明，验证代码基于Plonky3 STARK工具包以AIR电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在链下点对点代币交易场景中，需要在防止双重支付（即代币的并行状态）的同时，最小化区块链的复杂性和存储开销。传统链上验证方式成本高昂且吞吐受限，需要一种既保持信任性又具备高效性的基础设施。

Method: Unicity采用两层架构：聚合层（Layer 2风格服务）持有仅追加的键值存储，周期性通过传统区块链（共识层）认证其状态。每次状态变更时，聚合层向共识层提供关于键值存储追加一致性的密码学证明。具体使用Radix稀疏Merkle树（RSMT）生成每轮存储的加密摘要r_i，通过特殊类型的认证路径证明r_{i+1}仅由添加键值对得到。证明验证代码以代数中间表示（AIR）电路实现，运行在Plonky3 STARK工具包之上。

Result: 实现无需可信设置，在单台消费级CPU上达到每秒10,000次插入的吞吐量，验证时间在毫秒范围内。

Conclusion: Unicity以极低的区块链复杂性和存储开销实现了链下代币交易的双重支付防护，通过聚合层的密码学证明机制在保证信任无妥协的前提下达到了高效的吞吐量和快速的验证性能。

Abstract: Unicity is a novel blockchain infrastructure for enabling users to execute off-chain peer-to-peer token transactions while preventing parallel states of tokens (double-spending) with minimal blockchain complexity and storage. A key component of the infrastructure is the Aggregation Layer responsible for storing information about the spent states of tokens and providing compact cryptographic proofs of no double-spending for the users without making any compromises in trust. Aggregation Layer is a layer 2 style service that holds an append-only key-value repository that periodically certifies its state using a traditional blockchain that we call the Consensus Layer. Every time while certifying a changed state the Aggregation Layer provides a cryptographic proof to the Consensus Layer about the append-only consistency of the key-value store. We use the Radix Sparse Merkle Trees (RSMTs) to create the cryptographic digest r_i of the store in every round i and authentication paths of special type in order to prove that the next digest r_{i+1} was obtained while only adding some key-value pairs (k,v) to the repository. The proof verification code is implemented as an Algebraic Intermediate Representation (AIR) circuit on top of the Plonky3 STARK toolkit. Our implementation uses no trusted setup, achieves throughput of 10,000 insertions per second and and millisecond range verification time on a single consumer-class CPU.

</details>


### [219] [S12X Patch Diffing with QBinDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05350)
*Ben Gardiner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对商用车制动ECU的固件更新进行了逆向工程分析，通过分析更新程序提取固件，并对S12X架构固件镜像进行差分二进制分析，识别出召回中针对遗留协议处理中未记录漏洞的特定修复，并证明补丁功能存在关键缺陷，确认此次安全召回修复同时也是安全补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用车制动ECU的安全性至关重要，安全召回既可能是机械故障修复也可能涉及安全漏洞修补。作者试图通过逆向工程分析固件更新，揭示召回背后的安全隐患，特别是遗留协议处理中的未记录漏洞是否被修补，以及修补本身的质量如何。

Method: 作者对商用车辆制动ECU的固件更新程序进行逆向工程，从更新可执行文件中提取固件镜像，然后对S12X架构的固件版本进行差分二进制分析，对比更新前后的代码差异以定位特定更改。

Result: 研究识别出召回中针对遗留协议处理中未记录漏洞的具体修复改动，并发现修补后的功能仍包含关键缺陷，证明该安全召回不仅是机械修复，更是一次安全补丁。

Conclusion: 安全召回与安全补丁可以同一实施。然而，即便经过修补，固件仍存在关键缺陷，说明汽车ECU安全修复流程需要更严格的验证，且汽车行业应重视对固件更新做更深入的安全分析。

Abstract: This paper presents a reverse engineering analysis of a firmware update for a commercial vehicle Brake ECU. We analyze the updater executable to extract firmware and perform differential binary analysis of the S12X architecture firmware images. Our research identifies specific changes in the recall that address undocumented vulnerabilities in legacy protocol processing. We demonstrate that the patched functionality contained critical flaws. The findings confirm the safety recall remediation was also a security patch.

</details>


### [220] [Robust Context-Aware Detection of Malicious Instructions in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05430)
*Buzhao Liu,Xinhang Ma,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种兼具上下文与查询感知的恶意句子分类方法，并引入两种对抗训练变体（特征空间对抗训练与LLM改写模拟攻击）来抵御自适应规避攻击，在间接提示注入基准上优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM强大的指令遵循能力使其可作为自主代理的"大脑"，但也因此容易遭受间接提示注入（IPI）攻击——恶意指令被嵌入文本中。检测这类攻击的基础任务是正确分割文本为良性句子和恶意句子。现有检测方法缺乏查询相关的段落级检测能力，且未针对代理执行场景中可实现的自适应规避攻击进行加固。

Method: 提出了一个上下文与查询感知的恶意句子分类器。为增强其对规避攻击的鲁棒性，设计了两种对抗训练方法：(1) 特征空间对抗训练（AT），通过投影梯度优化在嵌入空间中近似规避攻击；(2) 基于LLM改写模拟可实现规避攻击的AT变体。两种变体均通过参数化实现效用与攻击鲁棒性之间的平滑权衡。

Result: 在间接提示注入基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在静态攻击下优于现有SOTA IPI防御基线；在自适应攻击下，对抗训练变体提供显著更高的效用、更低的攻击成功率，且常常两者兼得。此外，最优AT参数高度依赖应用领域，表明领域相关的恶意文本检测器调优在实践中是必要的。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了直接提示注入检测中的两个关键缺陷（缺乏查询相关的段落级检测和不具备自适应攻击鲁棒性），同时揭示了对抗训练参数与具体应用领域密切相关的特性，提示实际部署中需要按领域进行调整。

Abstract: The remarkable instruction-following ability of modern LLMs has enabled their practical use as the minds of agents that can autonomously complete increasingly complex tasks. Therein, however, also lies their vulnerability to attacks which embed malicious instructions in text, common variants of which are known as indirect prompt injection (IPI). A fundamental task in addressing this vulnerability is successful segmentation of a given text into benign and malicious sentences (if any). While a number of approaches for this task have been proposed, no detector combines query-relative detection at the segment level, and none are hardened against adaptive evasion attacks realizable in agentic executions. We address the former limitation by developing an approach for malicious sentence classification that is both context- and query-aware. Next, to harden the resulting classifier against evasion, we present two adversarial training methods. The first is directly adapted feature-space adversarial training (AT) in which evasions are approximated using projected-gradient-based optimization in the embedding space. The second simulates realizable evasion attacks in the AT loop through LLM-based paraphrasing. Crucially, we parametrize both AT variants to facilitate a smooth tradeoff between utility and attack robustness. In extensive experiments using indirect prompt injection benchmarks we show that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art IPI defense baselines under static attacks, while in the case of adaptive attacks, our AT variants provide significantly higher utility, lower attack success rate, and often both. Finally, we show that the best AT parameters can depend intimately on the particular application domain. Consequently, domain-dependent tuning of malicious text detectors is likely necessary in practice. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tavia-liu/CAD.

</details>


### [221] [Quantifying Bitcoin Network Resilience Through Critical Scenario Discovery: A Dual-Layer Framework for Discovering Contentious Fork Conditions in Decentralized Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05461)
*Peter Foytik,Sachin Shetty,Ross Gore,Eranga Bandara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用场景发现方法（基于PRIM的集成仿真）在真实bitcoind节点网络（Warnet）上对1,330个有效场景进行实验，揭示比特币争议性软分叉的结局主要由经济权重分配而非算力多数决定，并识别出关键的参数阈值区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的共识不仅依赖协议规则，还取决于具有不同经济利益的异构节点网络的涌现行为。争议性软分叉是干净解决还是产生持久链分裂，难以通过分析预测，但可以通过受控实验探索。

Method: 采用Scenario Discovery方法，应用Patient Rule Induction Method (PRIM)对集成仿真结果进行分析。使用Warnet运行真实bitcoind节点，覆盖1,330个有效场景，参数维度包括经济权重、矿池承诺、矿池意识形态、算力和难度调整区间。

Result: 发现经济权重分布是分叉结局的主要决定因素而非算力多数。具体三个发现：(1)经济支持下限(0.45-0.50)与覆盖上限(0.78-0.82)界定争议空间，经济自持续点(0.74)位于其间；(2)矿池承诺"翻转点"（承诺算力的0.214）——将最大矿池承诺给升级链反而逆转结果；(3)结局在算力和经济采纳两个独立层面上解决，受不同参数控制。个体用户节点在建模的经济权重下对分叉结局无可检测影响。

Conclusion: 论文提出三个可基于公开数据回答的监测问题，将阈值转化为操作指南，为比特币协议的争议性治理决策提供经验支持。

Abstract: Bitcoin's consensus depends not only on protocol rules but on the emergent behavior of a heterogeneous network of nodes with divergent economic stakes. Whether a contentious soft fork resolves cleanly or fractures into a persistent chain split is difficult to predict analytically but can be explored through controlled experimentation. This paper applies Scenario Discovery, an ensemble-simulation methodology using the Patient Rule Induction Method (PRIM), to identify the configuration regions that produce contentious fork outcomes. Using Warnet to run real bitcoind nodes across 1,330 valid scenarios spanning economic weight, mining-pool commitment, pool ideology, hashrate, and difficulty-retarget regime, we identify the parameter thresholds separating clean resolution from contested split. We show that economic weight distribution across a partition, not hashrate majority, is the primary determinant of resolution under Bitcoin's operational retarget interval and moderate price divergence. Three findings follow: an economic-support floor (0.45-0.50) and override ceiling (0.78-0.82) bound the contested space, with an Economic Self-Sustaining Point (0.74) between them; a pool-commitment "flip-point" (0.214 of committed hashrate) at which committing the largest pool to the upgrading chain paradoxically reverses the outcome; and outcomes resolve on two independent layers, hashrate and economic adoption, governed by different parameters. Individual user nodes show no detectable influence on fork outcomes under the modeled economic weightings. We close with three monitoring questions, answerable from public data, that translate these thresholds into operational guidance.

</details>


### [222] [Exploring Privacy Leakage and Data Disclosure Violations in the MacOS Application Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05474)
*Jyotirmay Chauhan,Kostas Solomos,Mir Masood Ali,Jason Polakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对macOS生态系统中规范数据收集与共享的机制进行了全面研究，开发了分析框架NutriScan，结合静态与动态分析技术，对1000个macOS应用进行动态分析，发现85%的应用在访问用户数据API时未披露，49.7%的应用向广告实体和托管提供商泄露数据，其中12.5%无相应披露。研究表明桌面应用正被在线追踪器利用来丰富用户画像和设备指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Web、移动和IoT生态系统的隐私风险已被广泛研究，但桌面环境长期以来被忽视。桌面应用仍被广泛使用，其隐私维度是重要却未充分研究的领域，因此需要对macOS生态系统中规范数据收集和共享实践的机制进行系统研究。

Method: 采用应用开发为中心的视角，梳理macOS中调解应用数据访问的各种机制及其交互关系。基于研究发现开发了NutriScan框架，整合静态和动态分析技术，创建macOS应用数据实践和披露的综合视图，并对1000个macOS应用进行动态分析。

Result: 85%的应用访问用户数据API但未披露；49.7%向广告实体和托管提供商泄露数据，其中12.5%无对应披露；发现macOS应用生态由具有不同数据抽象的分离机制组成，增加了开发者的复杂性并助长了未披露的隐私侵犯行为。

Conclusion: 桌面应用正在被在线追踪器利用以丰富用户画像和设备指纹，揭示了在线追踪生态的真实范围。作者提出了一系列缓解措施，旨在简化开发者的数据披露流程并改进苹果的应用审查过程。

Abstract: The systematic and excessive data collection practices of tech companies have rendered online privacy both a necessity and a sought-after commodity. However, while the privacy risks of the web, mobile, and IoT ecosystems have been extensively examined, desktop environments have been largely overlooked. As desktop apps continue to be widely used, they remain a critical yet understudied dimension of user privacy. In this paper, we address this gap by presenting the first, to our knowledge, comprehensive study of the mechanisms designed to regulate and disclose data collection and sharing practices in the macOS ecosystem. We adopt an app-development-centric view, and shed light on the interactions between the various macOS mechanisms that mediate apps' data access. Driven by our findings, we develop NutriScan, an analysis framework that incorporates both static and dynamic analysis techniques to create a consolidated view of macOS apps' data practices and disclosures. We use our system to dynamically analyze 1K macOS apps, and find that 85% of them access user-data APIs without disclosing it. 49.7% also exfiltrate data to advertising entities and hosting providers, 12.5% of which do so without a corresponding disclosure. We find that desktop apps are being leveraged by online trackers to enrich user profiles and device fingerprints, thus shedding new light on the true scope of the online tracking ecosystem. Our analysis reveals how the macOS app ecosystem is comprised of disjoint mechanisms with divergent data abstractions, thus increasing complexity for developers while also facilitating undisclosed privacy-invasive practices. Accordingly, we propose a series of mitigations that aim to both streamline the data disclosure process for developers and improve Apple's app vetting process.

</details>


### [223] [PromptShield Home: Ambient Multimodal Prompt Injection Defense for Smart-Home Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05495)
*He Zhang,Feilong Li,Dingning Long,Yilin Cui,Peijun Zhang,Yuewen Zhang,Qianyao Xu,Xinyi Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptShield-Home基准测试框架，用于评估智能家居多模态大语言模型（MLLM）助手能否区分真实用户命令与环境中看似命令的干扰内容（如电视语音、屏幕文字、旁人对话等）。实验对比三种抽象层（传统检测器L0、单一MLLM智能体L1、多智能体仲裁L2），发现检测器与MLLM范式以相反方式失败，且其正确集不相交——理想路由上限达94.1% vs 最佳单层76.5%。作者主张家居安全应依赖学习路由与传感器融合，而非用MLLM替换检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居助手广泛采用能直接感知视频和音频的多模态大语言模型，这引发了家庭场景特有的安全问题：智能体能否区分真实用户命令与来自环境或外部来源的、看似命令的内容（如电视语音、屏幕文字、旁听对话等）。现有研究缺乏针对这一问题的系统性基准与评估。

Method: 构建PromptShield-Home试点基准，涵盖寻址歧义、屏幕/音频注入、健康监测误触发、混合占用及合法命令基线等真实智能家居场景；对比三个抽象层：传统检测器（L0）、单一MLLM智能体（L1，含纯视觉、视觉+ASR、音视频三种配置）、多智能体调解（L2，含投票、角色专家、跨模型仲裁）；由于标签分布偏向"不行动"导致聚合准确率误导（常阻塞预测器得82%），分别报告不安全执行率和安全完成率。

Result: 两种范式以相反方式失败：检测器对所有内容都执行，而所有MLLM配置过度拒绝——几乎无法完成任何真实命令，且在每次真摔倒场景中都漏报。关键发现是两者的正确集不相交：理想化oracle总能选对层时达到94.1%，而最佳单层仅76.5%。作者将此作为上限而非系统（未实现路由器）。

Conclusion: 家庭智能体安全最好由学习路由和传感器融合来保障，而非用MLLM完全替代传统检测器。两种范式的互补性意味着设计一个能够在不同场景间智能切换层的路由机制，比追求单一模型的全能更有前景。

Abstract: Smart-home assistants increasingly use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that perceive video and audio directly. This raises a safety question specific to the home: can the agent tell a genuine user command from ambient or externally-sourced content, television speech, on-screen text, or an overheard conversation, that merely looks like a command? We introduce PromptShield-Home, a pilot benchmark of realistic smart-home scenarios spanning addressee ambiguity, screen/audio injection, health-monitor false triggers, mixed occupancy, and a legitimate-command floor, and use it to compare three abstraction layers: traditional detectors (L0), a single MLLM agent (L1; vision, vision+ASR, and audio-visual), and multi-agent mediation (L2; voting, role specialists, cross-model arbitration). Because the label distribution is skewed toward inaction, aggregate accuracy is misleading, a constant always-block predictor scores 82%, so we report unsafe-execution and safe-completion rates separately. The two paradigms fail in opposite ways: detectors act on everything, while every MLLM configuration over-refuses, completing almost no genuine command and missing a true fall in every case. Crucially, their correct sets are disjoint: an oracle that always picks the right layer reaches 94.1%, against 76.5% for the best single layer. We report this as an upper bound, not a system - no router is implemented - and argue that home-agent safety is best served by learned routing and sensor fusion, not by replacing detectors with an MLLM.

</details>


### [224] [Behavioral Residualization for Unsupervised Intrusion Detection in Automotive CAN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05548)
*Chandan Hegde,Mukundh R Reddy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出per-ID行为残差化（per-ID behavioral residualization）表示方法，用于CAN总线入侵检测。该方法从滑动窗口中提取14个时间、协议和载荷特征，并针对每个仲裁ID的正常基线进行残差化。在6种无监督检测器和2个数据集上验证表明，该表示方法而非单个检测器驱动性能提升，在真实的ROAD数据集上对重放合法ID的攻击达到recall>=0.99。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存的CAN入侵检测系统大多依赖存在性特征（如新出现的仲裁ID、冻结的载荷字节、异常的DLC值），这些特征在攻击容易分离的公开数据集上表现良好，但当攻击者重用合法仲裁ID时会失效。需要一种对真实攻击场景更具鲁棒性的表示方法。

Method: 提出per-ID行为残差化表示：从滑动窗口中提取14个时间、协议和载荷特征，然后将这些特征与每个仲裁ID的正常基线进行残差化处理，得到CAN特定表示。将该表示应用于6种无监督检测器进行入侵检测评估。

Result: 残差化在多数评估中提升平均F1（HCRL上21/24，ROAD上30/36，跨5个种子）。在更真实的ROAD数据集上，对重用合法ID的信号操控攻击实现recall>=0.99且高ROC-AUC。同时量化了两个局限：新ID泛洪（HCRL DoS，F1=0.02）和跨ID模糊测试（ROAD，F1=0.27）。

Conclusion: per-ID行为残差化是一种有效的CAN入侵检测表示方法，其性能提升主要来自表示本身而非特定检测器。该方法在重用合法ID的真实攻击场景下表现优异，但在新ID泛洪和跨ID模糊测试场景下存在明确的性能边界。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus, whose design prioritizes low cost and real-time performance but provides no message authentication or encryption. An attacker with physical or remote access can therefore inject arbitrary frames, making intrusion detection an important defense-in-depth mechanism.
  Most published CAN intrusion detection systems rely on presence-based features, such as novel arbitration IDs, frozen payload bytes, or anomalous DLC values. These features perform well on public datasets containing easily separable attacks but fail when attackers reuse legitimate arbitration IDs. We present per-ID behavioral residualization, a CAN-specific representation that extracts fourteen temporal, protocol, and payload features from sliding windows and residualizes them against each arbitration ID's normal baseline. Our central claim is that this representation, rather than any individual detector, drives the performance gains.
  Across six unsupervised detectors and two datasets, residualization improves mean F1 in the majority of evaluations (21/24 on HCRL and 30/36 on ROAD across five seeds). On the more realistic ROAD dataset, where attacks reuse legitimate IDs, the representation achieves recall >= 0.99 with high ROC-AUC on targeted signal-manipulation attacks. Two limitations are explicitly quantified: novel-ID flooding (HCRL DoS, F1 = 0.02) and cross-ID fuzzing (ROAD, F1 = 0.27), defining the measured coverage boundary of the proposed representation.

</details>


### [225] [A Self-Explainable Deep Architecture for Security Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05552)
*Ananth Shreekumar,Jyun-Jhu Syu,Muslum Ozgur Ozmen,Dongyan Xu,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: XSec是一种为安全应用设计的自解释深度架构，通过掩码方法提取判别性子特征并学习类原型，在测试时通过相似度层生成确定性、低延迟的解释，无需事后分析，在五个安全场景中达到97.33%的平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在安全应用中表现优异但缺乏可解释性，现有解释方法（可视化技术和事后方法）存在忠实度降低（局部近似误差）、不稳定（随机性依赖）和计算效率低（无法实时使用）等局限，亟需一种兼具高性能和高解释性的方案。

Method: XSec采用自解释架构：训练阶段通过新颖的掩码方法提取具有信息量的子特征，并学习代表每类特征的原型（prototype）；测试阶段使用专用相似度层（similarity layer）计算输入与原型间的相似度分数，直接生成可解释性的解释，无需事后分析（post-hoc）。

Result: 在五个多样化安全场景中评估，XSec达到97.33%的平均分类准确率且性能损失极小；对于固定的训练模型和输入，XSec产生确定性解释，并相比基于近似和扰动的解释方法显著降低了解释延迟。

Conclusion: XSec将自解释AI的适用性扩展到安全应用领域，弥合了深度学习性能与关键场景中可解释性需求之间的鸿沟，为安全领域的透明AI提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become integral to security applications due to their ability to model complex relationships in data and detect sophisticated threats. However, their complexity makes it difficult to understand how predictions are generated, posing significant challenges for interpretability, particularly in security applications where transparency is critical. Existing explanation methods, such as visual explanation techniques and post-hoc approaches, suffer from several limitations: reduced faithfulness due to local approximation errors, instability caused by reliance on randomness, and computational inefficiency that hinders real-time usage. To address these issues, we introduce XSec, a self-explainable deep architecture developed for security applications. During training, XSec uses a novel mask-based approach to extract informative sub-features from the data and learns prototypes, representative patterns that characterize each class. XSec then leverages the prototypes in a dedicated similarity layer at test time to compute similarity scores and generates interpretable explanations without the need for post-hoc analysis. We evaluate XSec across five diverse security scenarios, demonstrating its ability to achieve an average classification accuracy of 97.33% with minimal performance compromise. XSec produces deterministic explanations for a fixed trained model and input and substantially reduces explanation latency compared with approximation-based and perturbation-based post-hoc methods. Through this effort, we extend the applicability of self-explainable AI to security applications, bridging the gap between deep learning performance and the need for explainability in critical scenarios.

</details>


### [226] [When Experience Becomes Instruction: Trajectory Poisoning in Self-Evolving Agent Skill Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05563)
*Jialuo Chen,Lingqi Jiang,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Jianan Ma,Yunhao Feng,Yuqi Qing,Zhihao Yuan,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoisonedEvolution，一种针对自我进化技能（SES）系统中"轨迹提炼为技能"这一晋升过程的轨迹投毒攻击。在仅10%攻击者支持率下，该方法在SkillClaw的六个主流LLM进化器上实现了91.0%的成功嵌入率（546/600），并在结构不同的Trace2Skill流水线上实现了61.5%的迁移成功率（369/600），揭示了证据晋升过程是自进化智能体的安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SES系统将不可信的agent轨迹蒸馏为持久技能，实现"不可信经验变为可信指令"。但这种晋升机制可能被恶意投毒利用——攻击者可以通过注入有毒轨迹将目标行为嵌入技能库中。作者旨在揭示这一安全边界，填补自进化智能体安全研究的空白。

Method: 提出PoisonedEvolution攻击框架，采用"技能可见黑盒"模型：攻击者可检查目标技能并贡献有界证据，但无法观察私有池、进化逻辑或编辑技能库。攻击链包含三个必要条件：Inclusion（纳入）、Evolution Attribution（进化归因）和Realization（实现），其中归因是核心瓶颈——目标行为必须在晋升前表现出因果有用性、复发性和泛化性。作者使用惰性canary规格测试了四个代表性安全效应族，并在SkilClaw和Trace2Skill两个不同架构上进行评估。

Result: 在SkillClaw上10%攻击者支持下，546/600次试验（91.0% SER）成功嵌入目标行为；在Trace2Skill流水线上同比例下369/600次（61.5% SER）成功，证明跨进化架构的迁移性。受控研究中，30条记录批次中3条一致的攻击者记录即可实现嵌入，而单条记录效果显著较弱。消融实验识别出重复支持、因果框架和领域对齐编码是成功的主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 证据晋升机制是自进化智能体的安全边界。PoisonedEvolution表明，即使是具有有限可见性和证据贡献限制的黑盒攻击者，也能通过精心构造的轨迹投毒有效将目标行为嵌入技能库。这一发现凸显了在LLM智能体自进化系统中需要更强的安全防护机制。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill (SES) systems distill agent trajectories into persistent skills, allowing untrusted experience to become trusted instruction. We introduce PoisonedEvolution, a trajectory-poisoning attack on this promotion process. Our skill-visible black-box attacker can inspect a target skill and contribute bounded evidence, but cannot observe private pools or evolution logic or edit the skill bank. Artifact poisoning requires Inclusion, Evolution Attribution, and Realization. Attribution is the distinctive bottleneck: the target behavior must appear causally useful, recurrent, and generalizable before promotion. We evaluate four representative security-effect families using inert canary specifications. At 10% attacker support, across six mainstream LLM evolvers in SkillClaw, PoisonedEvolution embeds target behaviors in 546/600 trials (91.0% SER). On the structurally different Trace2Skill pipeline at the same ratio, it embeds target behaviors in 369/600 trials (61.5% SER), demonstrating transfer across evolution architectures. In a representative controlled study, three consistent attacker records suffice in a 30-record batch, whereas a single record is much weaker. Ablations identify recurring support, causal framing, and domain-aligned encoding as the main determinants of success. These findings expose evidence promotion as a security boundary for self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [227] [Detecting Safety Training Modification in Language Models via Activation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05578)
*Glen Messenger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍AMS（基于激活的模型扫描器），一种通过测量安全相关概念在激活空间中的几何结构来检测语言模型安全训练修改的工具。该工具在14个模型配置、4个架构家族和4类安全修改中验证，实现了71%的检测准确率，并将安全训练修改划分为四类分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源语言模型的广泛部署，存在多种方式可以修改或破坏模型的安全训练（如abliteration、uncensored微调等），但目前缺乏系统性的检测工具来识别这些修改。作者旨在通过测量激活空间中安全概念的几何结构来检测安全训练的修改，从而为模型安全保障提供自动化监控手段。

Method: AMS通过两个层次的检测机制工作：Tier 1使用sigma阈值检测训练移除（簇分离塌缩）和正交化abliteration（分离塌缩+拒绝方向旋转）两类修改；Tier 2使用方向相似性验证检测旋转但不塌缩的abliteration。方法基于测量安全相关概念在激活空间中的簇分离程度和拒绝方向的变化。

Result: 在14个模型配置中实现71%的留一交叉验证准确率；bootstrap 95%置信区间中位宽度为3.4 sigma；sigma值与行为合规性之间存在Pearson r = -0.546（p = 0.043）的方向性相关但噪声较大。提出了四类安全训练修改分类法，并识别出第四类（纯行为微调）无法通过激活探测检测这一已知失效模式。

Conclusion: AMS能够有效检测大多数常见的安全训练修改，但在检测纯行为层面的安全修改方面存在固有局限性。作者讨论了阈值校准、单次测量局限，并指出纯行为安全修改的检测是一个开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce AMS (Activation-based Model Scanner), a tool that detects modifications to safety training in language models by measuring the geometric structure of safety-relevant concepts in activation space. Safety training creates measurable separation between harmful and benign content classes; certain safety modifications collapse or rotate this structure, while others leave it intact. We validate AMS across 14 model configurations spanning 4 architecture families (Llama, Gemma, Qwen, Mistral) and four safety-modification categories (instruction-tuned, base, abliterated, uncensored fine-tunes). Leave-one-out cross-validation of thresholds achieves 71% accuracy (10/14); bootstrap 95% confidence intervals on sigma point estimates have median width 3.4 sigma. We measure behavioral compliance on 20 stratified JailbreakBench prompts per model and find that sigma on the harmful-content concept predicts compliance with Pearson r = -0.546 (p = 0.043), directionally but with meaningful noise. Mechanistic analysis identifies a four-class taxonomy of safety-training modifications distinguished by activation-space signature: training removal collapses cluster separation; weight-orthogonalization abliteration both collapses separation and rotates the refusal direction; rotation-without-collapse abliteration preserves separation while rotating direction; and behavioral fine-tuning preserves both magnitude and direction. AMS's Tier 1 sigma-threshold detects the first two classes; Tier 2 direction-similarity verification detects the third. The fourth is undetectable by activation-only probing and represents a documented failure mode. We discuss threshold calibration, limitations of single-run measurement, and the open problem of detecting behavioral-only safety modifications.

</details>


### [228] [Enhancing Anomaly Resilience in Research Networks: A Large-Scale Forecasting Benchmark for Dynamic Security Baselining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05605)
*Mohammad Arafath Uddin Shariff,Byrav Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个针对科研教育网络（RENs）的高保真流量预测框架，利用57天的Internet2骨干网数据（137亿包），系统评估了从SARIMA到TiDE、PatchTST等六种模型家族的960种实验配置。结果显示TiDE等先进架构将基线预测误差降低30-42%（p<0.001），并通过新颖的异常集成策略提升3.3%的鲁棒性，为区分科研工作流与网络攻击提供了首个统计验证的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RENs的正常流量模式——大规模突发性大象流，在统计上与DDoS等容量型攻击难以区分，导致传统监控系统产生高误报率，使安全运营人员无法识别真正的威胁。这一安全悖论需要建立动态安全基线来区分合法科研突发流量与潜在攻击。

Method: 利用57天Internet2骨干网数据集（覆盖10台骨干路由器、137亿数据包），开展首个大规模的异常感知流量预测模型基准测试。系统评估六种模型家族（从SARIMA到TiDE、PatchTST等长序列架构），共960种实验配置，并引入一种新颖的异常集成策略以增强模型在噪声环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: 先进的深度学习架构，尤其是TiDE，相比传统方法将基线预测误差降低了30-42%（p<0.001），显著改善了合法科研突发流量与潜在异常之间的区分能力。新颖的异常集成策略在噪声存在的情况下将模型鲁棒性提升了3.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个经过统计验证的框架，用于区分科研工作流与网络攻击，使REN安全运营更加自主和有韧性，为科研网络的安全监控奠定了新的基准确立方法。

Abstract: Research and Education Networks (RENs) serve as critical infrastructure for scientific discovery, yet they face a unique security paradox: their normal traffic patterns which are characterized by massive, bursty "elephant flows" are statistically indistinguishable from volumetric attacks such as DDoS to conventional monitoring systems. This similarity leads to high false-positive rates in anomaly detection, blinding security operators to genuine threats. In this paper, we propose and evaluate a high-fidelity traffic forecasting framework designed to establish dynamic security baselines for RENs. Leveraging an exclusive 57-day Internet2 dataset spanning ten backbone routers (13.7 billion packets), we perform the first large-scale benchmark of anomaly-aware forecasting models in this domain. We systematically evaluate six model families, from SARIMA to state-of-the-art long-sequence architectures (TiDE, PatchTST), across 960 experimental configurations. Our results demonstrate that these advanced architectures, particularly TiDE, reduce baseline prediction error by 30-42% compared to traditional methods ($p < 0.001$), significantly improving the distinction between legitimate scientific bursts and potential anomalies. Furthermore, we introduce a novel anomaly-integration strategy that improves model robustness by 3.3% in the presence of noise. This work provides the first statistically validated framework for distinguishing scientific workflows from network attacks, enabling more autonomous and resilient network security operations.

</details>


### [229] [Breaking Customized LLMs for Coding: Automated Red Teaming for Instruction Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05659)
*Yuchen Chen,Wei Cheng,Yuan Xiao,Wising Sun,Chunrong Fang,Yang Liu,Zhenyu Chen,Baowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIA，一种自动化的红队框架，用于针对定制化LLM构建隐蔽且有效的指令后门攻击。ARIA利用攻击者LLM迭代生成和优化后门指令，通过目标LLM的结构化反馈（隐蔽性、干净任务效用、后门有效性）进行引导。在三个代码智能任务、四个代表性LLM上的实验表明，ARIA实现了最高0.945的攻击成功率，同时保持最佳干净任务效用，在规避平台侧和用户侧检测方面显著优于现有攻击（漏报率高达1.000），并对现有防御方法保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM定制平台允许用户通过系统提示中的指令嵌入构建任务特定模型，但引入了新的攻击面：指令后门攻击。现有攻击存在两个关键限制：依赖容易被检测的显式触发模式，以及需要大量人工努力来手工构建任务特定的后门指令，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: ARIA框架利用一个攻击者LLM迭代地生成和细化后门指令，由目标LLM沿三个维度提供结构化反馈：隐蔽性（stealthiness）、干净任务效用（clean-task utility）和后门有效性（backdoor effectiveness），形成一个自动化优化的闭环。

Result: 在三个代码智能任务和四个代表性LLM上的实验表明，ARIA实现了最高0.945的攻击成功率（ASR），同时在各任务中保持最佳干净任务效用。ARIA在多种编程语言上具有良好的泛化能力，对生成温度保持鲁棒性，在规避平台侧和用户侧检测方面达到最高1.000的假阴性率，并有效规避现有防御方法。

Conclusion: ARIA证明了自动化生成隐蔽且有效的指令后门攻击是可行的，显著优于现有基线攻击。该工作揭示了定制化LLM平台面临的新安全威胁，强调了构建更强防御机制的必要性，并展示了在攻击性测试框架中利用LLM反馈驱动迭代优化的有效性。

Abstract: LLM customization platforms allow users to build task-specific models for code intelligence tasks by embedding instructions into system prompts, without modifying the underlying model parameters. While these platforms lower the barrier to developing customized LLMs, they also introduce a new attack surface: instruction backdoor attacks, in which adversaries implant hidden malicious behaviors into customized instructions. However, existing attacks suffer from two key limitations. First, they often rely on explicit trigger patterns readily detected by platform-side or user-side inspection. Second, they require substantial manual effort to craft task-specific backdoored instructions, limiting their scalability.
  In this paper, we propose ARIA, an automated red-teaming framework for crafting covert and effective backdoored instructions against customized LLMs. ARIA leverages an attacker LLM to iteratively generate and refine backdoored instructions, guided by structured feedback from the target LLM along three dimensions: stealthiness, clean-task utility, and backdoor effectiveness. We evaluate ARIA on three code intelligence tasks, using four representative LLMs, and compare it with three baseline attacks. Experimental results show that ARIA achieves the highest attack success rate of 0.945, while maintaining the best clean-task utility across all tasks. ARIA also generalizes well across programming languages and remains robust to generation temperature. Furthermore, ARIA significantly outperforms existing attacks in evading platform-side and user-side detection, achieving a false negative rate of up to 1.000, and stays effective against existing defense methods, demonstrating its strong generalizability and robustness.

</details>


### [230] [Algebraic Cryptanalytic Extraction on Hard-Label Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05736)
*Zirui Chen,Shi Tang,Zhengchao Gao,Yongjia Su,Lingyue Qin,Xiaoyang Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出近似签名向量（ASV）方法，将Carlini等人（EUROCRYPT 2025）在硬标签设置下的FCNN模型提取攻击从几何视角转化为代数框架，用内积运算代替SVD秩检查，将聚类复杂度从O(n²·(d^(k))³)降至O(n·(d^(k))³)；同时首次实现了针对硬标签最大池化CNN的模型提取攻击，通过基于核的聚类方案充分利用卷积权值共享特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carlini等人在EUROCRYPT 2025提出的硬标签设置下最先进的神经网络模型提取攻击虽然理论上具有多项式时间复杂度，但其双点聚类依赖SVD，时间复杂度为O(n²·(d^(k))³)，导致实际运行时间巨大，成为计算瓶颈。此外，硬标签设置下的最大池化CNN模型提取攻击在密码学分析层面仍属空白。

Method: 本文提出ASV方法，基于两个关键观察：（1）高维随机向量近似正交；（2）实际DNN中的神经元倾向于学习解纠缠特征。ASV用简单内积运算替代SVD基的秩检查，将FCNN聚类复杂度降至O(n·(d^(k))³)。对于CNN，提出改进的ASV方法，采用基于核（kernel-centric）的聚类方案而非基于神经元（neuron-centric）的方案，充分利用卷积中的权值共享特性。

Result: 在64-64×4-10 FCNN和带最大池化的LeNet-5（CNN）上的实验表明，ASV方法大幅削减了聚类时间，并提高了模型提取的整体效率。

Conclusion: ASV方法在保持模型提取攻击有效性的同时，显著提升了计算效率，填补了硬标签最大池化CNN模型提取攻击的研究空白，为该领域提供了更实用的攻击方案。

Abstract: Although the state-of-the-art neural network model extraction attack in the hard-label setting by Carlini et al. at EUROCRYPT 2025 has polynomial-time complexity in theory, its dual-point clustering relies on singular value decomposition (SVD) with a time complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n^2 \cdot (d^{(k)})^3)$, resulting in huge runtime in practice. To address this computational bottleneck, this work transforms Carlini et al.'s geometric-view hard-label attack into an algebraic framework, and proposes a novel Approximate Signature Vector (ASV) method to achieve efficient parameter extraction on Fully Connected Neural Networks (FCNNs) by leveraging two key observations: high-dimensional random vectors are nearly orthogonal, and neurons in practical DNNs tend to learn disentangled features. The proposed ASV method replaces SVD-based rank checking with simple inner-product operations, reducing the clustering complexity to $\mathcal{O}(n \cdot (d^{(k)})^3)$ on average. Furthermore, this paper presents the first model extraction attack against hard-label max-pooling Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) by proposing an advanced ASV method with a kernel-centric clustering scheme instead of the neuron-centric clustering, which fully exploits the property of weight sharing in convolutions and fills the cryptanalysis gap. Experiments on a 64-64$\times$4-10 FCNN and LeNet-5 (CNN) with max pooling demonstrate that our ASV method drastically cuts clustering time, and improves the overall efficiency in the model extraction.

</details>


### [231] [ABC: Numerical Data Collection under Local Differential Privacy without Prior Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05737)
*Incheol Baek,Hyungbin Kim,Yon Dohn Chung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应本地差分隐私（LDP）框架——ABC方法，通过用户发送扰动数值和被剪裁信号，迭代调整数据域范围以适应未知的数据分布，无需预先知道数据域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP机制需要预定义数据域，但实际中数据域往往未知。域过窄会导致值被剪裁造成信息损失，域过宽则引入过多噪声降低数据质量，因此需要动态估计数据域的方法。

Method: 每个用户发送两部分信息：扰动后的数值数据，以及一个私有化信号指示其原始值是否被当前域剪裁。通过聚合这些信号，ABC方法迭代调整剪裁边界以适应底层数据分布，理论分析证明估计的数据域收敛到合适范围。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架在不同数据集和不同LDP机制上显著提升了数值数据收集质量，估计的数据范围在实践中成功收敛，且通过消融研究验证了方法对超参数具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ABC方法解决了LDP中数据域未知的关键挑战，通过自适应调整域范围在无需先验知识的情况下提升数据收集质量，并具备理论收敛保证和实际鲁棒性。

Abstract: Local Differential Privacy (LDP) provides strong privacy guarantees for collecting numerical data. A fundamental challenge, however, is that existing LDP mechanisms require a predefined data domain, which is often unknown in practice. This lack of prior knowledge creates a critical dilemma for the data collector: if the chosen domain is too narrow, values outside the range are clipped, leading to information loss. Conversely, if the domain is too wide, excessive noise is added during the privatization process, which degrades the quality of collected data. This highlights the need for methods that can dynamically estimate the data domain.
  In this work, we propose an adaptive LDP framework that addresses this problem. In our method, each user sends two pieces of information: their perturbed numerical data, and a privatized signal indicating if their original value was clipped by the current domain. By aggregating these signals, our proposed method, Adaptive Bounding of Clipping regions (ABC) method, iteratively adjusts the domain to fit the underlying data distribution without prior knowledge. Our theoretical analysis shows that the estimated data domain converges to an appropriate range.
  In the empirical evaluation, the results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves the quality of numerical data collection across various datasets and underlying LDP mechanisms. We also show that the estimated range successfully converges in practice and our approach is robust to its hyperparameters through comprehensive ablation studies.

</details>


### [232] [On the Figures of Merit for Quantum Software Security: Toward a Benchmarking Rubric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05831)
*Badhon Rahman,Majid Haghparast,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出量子软件安全需要像性能指标（规模、质量、速度）一样受到重视，定义了一套结构化的安全性能指标（S-FoMs），并通过ISO/IEC 25010安全子特性进行组织，同时提出量子软件安全态势（QSSP）评分作为统一的量化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子软件通过多租户和云端的QaaS堆栈提供，针对管道各个阶段的安全攻击向量日益受到关注。尽管社区已经建立了Scale、Quality、Speed三大性能指标，以及部分软件质量指标，但量子软件的安全仍缺乏统一、可比的量化度量标准。已有的TVD和DFC等安全指标是针对特定电路混淆技术临时引入的，彼此不兼容，亟需系统性的安全性能基准。
