Method: 论文采取三方面方法：(i) 刻画三层测量差距（three-layer measurement gap）；(ii) 提出结构化的S-FoM集合，按ISO/IEC 25010安全子特性、QaaS管道映射和测量成熟度进行组织；(iii) 定义基准评估准则，将S-FoMs归一化并聚合成综合的量子软件安全态势（QSSP）评分。此外还提供了对已发表混淆技术的说明性再分析。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整的安全性能指标框架S-FoMs和一个聚合的QSSP评分体系，并通过重新分析已发表的混淆技术进行了说明性验证，展示了该框架的适用性和跨技术可比性。

Conclusion: 本文旨在迈出量子软件堆栈（QSS）安全感知基准化的第一步，强调量子软件的安全应获得与性能同等程度的关注和系统化度量。

Abstract: Quantum software is increasingly provided through multi-tenant and cloud-based Quantum-as-a-Service (QaaS) stacks. A growing concern about the diverse attack vectors across the pipeline has been demonstrated in recent research. Yet the community has converged on three mature pillars: Scale (Qubit Count), Quality (Quantum Volume), and Speed (Circuit Layer Operations per Second (CLOPS)) for the merit performance figures. Moreover, it has also begun to define software-quality metrics. However, the security of quantum software remains largely unmeasured. A few quantitative security indicators, such as Total Variation Distance (TVD) and Degree of Functional Corruption (DFC), exist. Although they were introduced ad hoc for individual circuit obfuscation techniques, they are incompatible. We assert that the security of quantum software deserves the same attention as the performance: an explicit set of Security Figures of Merit (S-FoMs). The research of this paper is threefold: (i) characterizes a three-layer measurement gap, (ii) proposes a structured S-FoM set organized by ISO/IEC 25010 security sub-characteristics, QaaS pipeline mapping, and measurement maturity, and (iii) defines a benchmarking rubric that normalizes and aggregates S-FoMs into a combined Quantum Software Security Posture (QSSP) score. Additionally, an illustrative reanalysis of published obfuscation techniques has been presented. Our aim is a first step toward security-aware benchmarking of the Quantum Software Stack (QSS).

</details>


### [233] [An End-to-End Threat Model for the Quantum-as-a-Service Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05836)
*Badhon Rahman,Majid Haghparast,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对量子即服务（QaaS）平台的安全威胁，将工作流分解为六阶段模型，并应用STRIDE威胁建模方法，系统地梳理了各阶段的攻击向量，识别了三个跨阶段高影响攻击链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子计算安全研究中的攻击（如校准篡改、SWAP攻击、QubitHammer等）各自独立、术语分散，缺乏针对QaaS堆栈结构的系统性威胁建模框架，导致安全分析不完整。

Method: 将QaaS工作流分解为六阶段模型，并对每个阶段应用STRIDE威胁建模，构建攻击向量矩阵，覆盖量子特定、传统继承和合理（plausible）三个安全层级；同时深入研究STRIDE中较少被探讨的否认（repudiation）和权限提升（elevation-of-privilege）类别，并识别三种跨阶段攻击链。

Result: 提出了一个覆盖整个QaaS堆栈的结构化威胁模型矩阵，揭示了各阶段量子特定、传统继承及合理攻击向量，并识别出三个具有更高影响等级的跨阶段攻击链。

Conclusion: 该六阶段STRIDE威胁建模框架为QaaS平台提供了统一、结构化的安全分析视角，弥补了现有量子安全研究中攻击碎片化、缺乏系统性堆栈级建模的不足。

Abstract: Cloud-based accessing of Quantum-as-a-Service (QaaS) platforms such as IBM Quantum, IonQ Cloud, and Amazon Braket is becoming popular day by day. Hybrid quantum-classical algorithms (VQE, QAOA, QML) transfer data via a long layered pipeline of orchestration, compilation, and execution. Recent works have demonstrated various critical attacks at individual stages: Calibration tampering, SWAP attacks, QubitHammer, and so on. However, these attacks remain separated because of their own terminology, and existing STRIDE-based threat modeling in the context of quantum lacks a structured view towards the QaaS stack itself. We address this concern by decomposing the workflow into six-stage model with STRIDE threat modeling. Our matrix demonstrated attack vectors in quantum-specific, inherited classical, and plausible tiers for each of the stages. We further investigate the underexplored sections (repudiation and elevation-of-privilege) and distinguish three different cross-stage attack chains with higher impacts.

</details>


### [234] [RustGo: Fairly Directed Greybox Fuzzing for Enforcing Rust Memory Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05870)
*Dongyeon Yu,Jiun Min,Yewan Na,Mijung Kim,Taegyu Kim,Yuseok Jeon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RustGo，一个针对Rust语言的定向灰盒模糊测试工具，通过Rust特有的静态分析自动识别潜在内存漏洞目标并精准剪枝无关路径，聚焦于unsafe Rust代码区域进行高效模糊测试。实验表明，RustGo平均剪枝78.49%的无关路径，到达目标速度比现有模糊器快2.09至5.08倍，并发现了13个未知漏洞（其中6个获得RUSTSEC编号，1个获得CVE编号）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust通过所有权等严格安全策略保障内存安全，但unsafe Rust代码区域未被这些策略完全覆盖，仍可能产生内存漏洞。现有模糊测试方法对整个代码（包括已被编译器保障安全的safe Rust）进行模糊测试，导致模糊资源的低效利用。因此，需要一种能够聚焦于unsafe代码区域、公平高效分配模糊资源的测试方法。

Method: RustGo提出了一种新的Rust定向灰盒模糊器，其方法包括：(1) 利用Rust特有的静态分析自动识别潜在内存漏洞目标；(2) 精确剪枝与每个目标无关的路径；(3) 针对每个识别出的目标，采用独立状态维护和动态剪枝的模糊测试策略，以实现平衡且聚焦的模糊测试。

Result: 在多种真实世界Rust应用上的评估表明，RustGo平均剪枝78.49%的无关路径，到达目标的速度比现有模糊器快2.09至5.08倍，并成功识别出13个未知漏洞（其中6个被分配RUSTSEC ID，1个被分配CVE ID）。

Conclusion: RustGo通过聚焦于unsafe Rust代码区域并动态剪枝无关路径，有效提升了Rust模糊测试的效率与公平性，证明了定向模糊测试在Rust生态中的实用性，同时揭示了此类代码中仍存在实际安全风险，需要更针对性的测试工具。

Abstract: Rust is a popular systems programming language that provides strong memory safety and introduces low-performance overhead. While Rust guarantees memory safety through strict security policies, such as ownership, memory bugs can still occur in unsafe-related Rust codes where these policies are not fully enforced. Although such unsafe Rust code accounts for only a small portion of the entire code (e.g., 10%), existing approaches fuzz the entire code-including safe Rust, whose memory safety is already enforced by the Rust compiler-resulting in inefficient use of fuzzing resources. In this paper, we propose RustGo, the new Rust-directed greybox fuzzer that effectively and fairly focuses on code regions potentially containing memory bugs. For this, RustGo automatically identifies potential memory bug targets and accurately prunes the paths irrelevant to each target by leveraging Rust-specific static analysis. For each identified target, RustGo includes a new fuzzing approach that maintains an independent state and applies dynamic pruning to maximize balanced and focused fuzzing. We evaluate RustGo on various real-world Rust applications. On average, RustGo prunes 78.49% of irrelevant paths, reaches targets x 2.09 to x 5.08 faster than existing fuzzers, and identifies 13 unknown bugs (six assigned RUSTSEC IDs and one assigned CVE ID).

</details>


### [235] [The Vulnerability With No CVE: Managing Persistent Gaps Between Mandate and Authority in AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05884)
*Shayell Aharon Salomon Amir Shaked Matan Noga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体姿态漏洞（APV）"作为一种面向持久的、跨组件的智能体控制暴露的漏洞管理抽象，将运行时的多种表现与不变的姿态关联起来，直到授权收紧、控制补齐、风险被接受或闭环验证后才关闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指南已识别出过度代理、过度权限、弱任务绑定授权和智能体控制不足等风险，控制框架也描述了约束、授权、观察、验证和响应的能力。但安全项目仍缺乏一种方法来管理跨越组件、超越单次事件的持久部署实例。

Method: 提出APV作为任务条件化的漏洞管理抽象，是一种针对组合智能体控制暴露的持久记录。明确将其与CVE产品缺陷、OWASP Excessive Agency、Agent Baseline控制结果及运行时授权-执行差距相区分，并提供了领域实例、阈值化定义、六种反复出现的APV模式、漏洞生命周期、最小记录、控制与闭环矩阵、工具启示和可测试的研究议程。

Result: 提供了APV的完整概念框架，包括六种反复出现的APV模式、漏洞生命周期、最小记录结构、控制与闭环矩阵，以及工具化启示和可测试的研究议程。

Conclusion: APV并非新的根本原因风险类别，而是对现有过度代理、授权和控制组合弱点的操作性落实，旨在弥补现有控制框架在管理持久部署智能体实例方面的空白。

Abstract: Existing guidance identifies excessive agency, excessive permission, weak task-bound authorization, and inadequate agent controls as important risks. Control frameworks also describe capabilities for constraining, authorizing, observing, validating, and responding to agent activity. Yet security programs still need a way to manage persistent deployed instances that span components and outlive any one event.
  We propose the agentic posture vulnerability (APV) as a task-conditioned vulnerability-management abstraction: a durable record for a composed agent-control exposure. One posture may produce different runtime manifestations across tasks; APV links those manifestations to the invariant posture and remains open until authority is narrowed, a missing control is added, risk is accepted, or closure is verified. APV is not proposed as a new root-cause class of risk; it operationalizes existing excessive-agency, authorization, and control-composition weaknesses.
  We distinguish APVs from CVE-addressable product defects, OWASP Excessive Agency, Agent Baseline control outcomes, and the runtime authorization-execution gap. We then provide a field vignette, a thresholded definition, six recurring APV patterns, a vulnerability lifecycle, a minimum record, a control-and-closure matrix, tooling implications, and a testable research agenda.

</details>


### [236] [Tool Demo: Topology analysis with GPML for detection of cyberattacks in Water Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05902)
*Majed Jaber,Abdul Qadir Khan,Ankush Meshram,Julien Michel,Côme Frappé - - Vialatoux,Pierre Parrend*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拓扑驱动的方法，利用图处理机器学习（GPML）框架检测配水网络中的网络攻击。该方法将原始流量转化为动态图，提取社区和谱指标分析结构变化，并在三个工业数据集（HITL、SWaT、CrossTest）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配水网络依赖工业控制系统集成物理过程与通信网络，容易遭受改变流量模式与网络行为的网络攻击。传统检测方法依赖原始流量或协议信息，往往忽略攻击引发的结构变化，因此需要一种能捕捉拓扑结构变化的检测方法。

Method: 采用拓扑驱动的检测方法，基于图处理机器学习（GPML）框架。将原始流量转换为动态图，从中提取社区（community）和谱（spectral）指标，并随时间分析网络结构和通信的修改情况。

Result: 该方法在三个工业配水数据集（HITL、SWaT、CrossTest）上进行评估，谱和社区图指标显著提升了模型对网络攻击和物理攻击的检测性能。

Conclusion: 基于图拓扑的动态图指标（社区与谱指标）能有效补充传统流量层面的检测方法，提升配水网络工业控制系统中网络与物理攻击的检测能力。

Abstract: Water distribution networks depends on industrial control systems to integrate the physical process with communication network, making them vulnerable to cyberattacks that alter the traffic pattern and network behavior. Traditional detection approaches that rely on raw traffic or protocol information often oversee structural changes that are induced by such attacks. In this work, we presents a topology-driven approach for detection of cyberattacks in water distribution networks based on Graph Processing for Machine Learning (GPML) framework. The raw traffic is transformed into dynamic graphs, from which community and spectral metrics are extracted and analyzed for any structural and communication modifications with time. The proposed methodology is evaluated on three industrial water distribution datasets such as HITL, SWaT, and CrossTest. Spectral and community graph metrics improve the model performance in detection of cyber and pyhiscal attacks across the three datasets.

</details>


### [237] [MMAligner: Safeguarding Multimodal Large Language Models through Representation Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05909)
*Shenyi Zhang,Keyan Guo,Zihao Wang,Xuebin Li,Lingchen Zhao,Hongxin Hu,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）安全退化的根本原因——多模态不安全输入经过表示偏移（representation shift）后落在已有拒绝边界之外，而非模型缺乏安全能力。基于此，提出MMAligner方法，将不安全多模态表示校准回既有的拒绝区域，以极小的训练数据和效用损失（<2%）将不安全输入的平均拒答率提升至99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs存在安全不一致性：面对语义等价的文本提示会拒答，但面对多模态输入却产生有害响应。已有防御方案要么依赖外部护栏（增加推理开销且不修复本质缺陷），要么进行安全微调（黑盒优化可能牺牲效用或需要大规模多模态数据集）。作者希望从根源上解释这种安全分歧，并设计一种不依赖外部模块、不牺牲效用的内在对齐方法。

Method: 首先对MLLM表示进行几何分析，发现文本中学习到的安全子空间和拒绝边界在多模态下依然有效，但多模态不安全输入发生了表示偏移，使其大多落在拒绝边界之外。基于此发现提出MMAligner，包含三个目标：硬下界确保拒绝行为、软上界避免过度修改表示、良性输入保持目标以不损害效用，将不安全多模态表示校准回既有的拒绝区域。

Result: 在多个开源MLLM上的实验表明，MMAligner将不安全多模态输入的平均拒答率提升到99%，效用损失低于2%，且仅需极少量训练数据，在安全-效用权衡上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 多模态安全退化源于表示错位而非安全能力缺失；通过将不安全多模态表示校准回既有的拒答区域，可以高效地恢复模型内在的安全机制，实现安全性与效用性的更优平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often refuse unsafe text prompts yet generate harmful responses to semantically equivalent multimodal inputs. Existing defenses either rely on external guardrails, which add inference overhead without repairing intrinsic flaws, or safety fine-tuning, which treats alignment as black-box optimization and may sacrifice utility or require large multimodal datasets. To identify the cause of this safety disparity, we analyze MLLM representations geometrically. We find that safety mechanisms learned from text persist across modalities: a shared safety subspace and refusal boundary remain effective, and representations inside this boundary consistently trigger refusals. However, unsafe multimodal inputs undergo a representation shift that places most of them outside the boundary, allowing them to bypass the model's intrinsic safety mechanism. This indicates that multimodal safety degradation stems from representation misalignment rather than the absence of safety capability. Based on this finding, we propose MMAligner, a safeguarding method that calibrates unsafe multimodal representations into the pre-existing refusal region. MMAligner applies a hard lower bound to ensure refusal, a soft upper bound to avoid excessive modification, and a preservation objective for benign inputs. Experiments across multiple open-source MLLMs show that MMAligner raises the average refusal rate on unsafe multimodal inputs to 99% with less than 2% utility degradation and minimal training data, substantially improving the safety-utility trade-off over existing baselines. (*Due to the notification from arXiv, "The Abstract field cannot be longer than 1,920 characters", the Abstract that appeared is shortened.)

</details>


### [238] [A Note on the Influence of a Zero Length Nonce on GCM and GMAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06061)
*Yaobin Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种针对GCM和GMAC的简单攻击，利用零长度nonce可以恢复哈希密钥，从而允许攻击者任意伪造密文或消息。该攻击适用于ISO/IEC版本（允许零长度nonce），但不适用于NIST版本（要求nonce至少为1位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GCM和GMAC是广泛使用的认证加密和消息认证码方案，其安全性依赖于哈希密钥的保密性。作者发现ISO/IEC标准版本允许空nonce，这为一个简单攻击创造了条件。

Method: 利用零长度nonce（空字符串）构造特定的输入条件，通过简单的代数运算恢复GCM/GMAC的哈希密钥（hash key）。一旦密钥被恢复，攻击者即可伪造任意密文或消息。

Result: 攻击可以成功恢复哈希密钥，使攻击者能够完全伪造任意密文或消息。攻击对ISO/IEC版本有效，但对NIST版本无效，因为NIST明确要求nonce至少为一bit。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了ISO/IEC版本的GCM和GMAC因允许零长度nonce而存在严重安全漏洞，建议标准制定者应避免允许空nonce，采用与NIST版本一致的至少一bit的nonce长度要求。

Abstract: In this note, we show a simple attack that can recover the hash key of GCM and GMAC by using a zero length nonce. After recovering the hash key, the adversary can forge an arbitrary ciphertext or message as she wants. We note that the ISO/IEC version of GCM and GMAC allows the nonce to be a zero length string, while the NIST version of GCM and GMAC explicitly requires the nonce to be at least one bit. Hence, our attack works for the ISO/IEC version and cannot work for the NIST version.

</details>


### [239] [dfence: Fine-Grained Speculation Barriers for Efficient and Effective Hardware-Software Protection in the Spectre Era (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06124)
*Davide Davoli,Marton Bognar,Lesly-Ann Daniel,Benjamin Grégoire,Frank Piessens,Tamara Rezk*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出dfence，一种新的CPU指令，通过泛化软件缓解方法Speculative Load Hardening (SLH)来同时缓解Spectre-PHT和Spectre-STL两种投机执行攻击，并设计一个类型系统来静态验证dfence指令的正确放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机执行攻击（如Spectre-PHT和Spectre-STL）仍是现代处理器中的关键安全威胁。现有软件缓解方案SLH仅能防御Spectre-PHT且需软件管理的投机掩码，代价高且易出错；针对Spectre-STL的防御（如SSBD位）则带来额外性能开销且缺乏细粒度控制。

Method: 作者提出dfence这一新型CPU指令，将SLH泛化以同时缓解两种攻击。开发者可对敏感寄存器进行标注，硬件确保这些值不会在投机执行中泄露。该指令在Proteus CPU中实现，并设计了一个类型系统用于静态验证dfence指令在代码中的正确放置。

Result: 在Proteus CPU上实现了dfence，安全性和性能评估显示基准测试平均性能开销小于1%。

Conclusion: dfence以极小的硬件支持和性能开销，同时有效缓解Spectre-PHT和Spectre-STL两类投机执行攻击，并通过类型系统保证其安全采用，为投机执行攻击防御提供了硬件-软件协同的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative execution attacks such as Spectre-PHT and Spectre-STL remain a critical security concern in modern processors. While software-based mitigations like Speculative Load Hardening (SLH) offer effective protection against Spectre-PHT, they are limited in scope and require software-managed speculative masks, which can be error-prone and costly. Defenses against Spectre-STL, such as the Speculative Store Bypass Disable bit (SSBD), incur additional performance overhead and lack fine-grained control. In this work, we introduce dfence, a new CPU instruction that generalizes SLH to mitigate both Spectre-PHT and Spectre-STL with minimal hardware support. dfence enables developers to annotate sensitive registers, with the hardware ensuring that these values do not leak transiently. We implement dfence in the Proteus CPU and evaluate its security and performance, demonstrating less than 1% average performance overhead for our benchmarks. In addition, to support easy and secure adoption, we design a type system that statically verifies the correct placement of dfence instructions in code.

</details>


### [240] [Hardware Keystores for AI Agent Signing Workflows: A Zero-Trust MCP Enforcement Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06130)
*Leo Sambrook,Sampo Sovio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出用硬件密钥库（HSM/TPM/智能卡）替代软件驻留的私钥，并通过PKCS#11接口暴露，配合五层零信任架构，将LLM智能体被注入攻击时的密钥窃取成功率从19.3%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体执行密码学操作（如签署Git提交、API认证、签发证书）时，私钥存储在纯文本文件、环境变量或容器内存等软件可访问位置，任何具有足够读取权限的进程都可提取原始密钥材料。实际生产事故表明，通过电子邮件注入可在5分钟内从广泛部署的框架中窃取私钥，迫切需要同时保证密钥机密性和密钥使用的内容感知授权。

Method: 将软件驻留密钥替换为硬件驻留密钥，通过供应商中立的PKCS#11接口访问；硬件密钥库（HSM、TPM、智能卡）在设备上执行密码操作，主机仅通过不透明句柄接收结果。硬件隔离为核心贡献，由其周围的五层零信任强制栈支撑：会话身份（SAGA）、作用域边界（Smax）、语义验证（RAV）、污点跟踪和硬件执行边界。

Result: 基于AgentDojo ImportantInstructionsAttack模板的12种注入场景进行评估，测试4个LLM模型（gpt-oss-120b、Qwen2.5-72B、DeepSeek-V4-Flash，共n=192）。基线模式下三个模型跟随注入指令，攻击成功率（ASR）为19.3%（95%CI: 14.3%-25.4%）；受保护模式下ASR为0%（Wilson 95%CI上限2.0%），且在4个良性任务场景中零误报。

Conclusion: 硬件密钥驻留结合五层零信任强制栈，可在不牺牲可用性的前提下有效抵御LLM智能体面临的注入攻击导致的密钥窃取，实现密钥机密性与内容感知授权的双重目标。

Abstract: AI agents performing cryptographic operations (signing Git commits, authenticating API calls, issuing certificates) currently store private keys in software-accessible locations: plaintext files, environment variables, or container memory. Any process with sufficient read privileges can extract the raw key material. A recent production incident demonstrated the practical severity: private keys were exfiltrated from a widely deployed framework via email injection in under five minutes. We aim to enforce both key confidentiality and content-aware authorisation for key use. To that end, we replace software-resident keys with hardware-confined keys accessible through a vendor-neutral PKCS#11 interface. A hardware keystore (HSM, TPM, smart card) executes cryptographic operations on-device; the host receives only the result via opaque handles. Hardware confinement is the primary contribution; it is enabled by a surrounding five-layer Zero-Trust enforcement stack comprising session identity (SAGA), scope bounds (Smax), semantic validation (RAV), taint tracking, and the hardware execution boundary. We evaluate against 12 injection scenarios derived from AgentDojo's ImportantInstructionsAttack template (Debenedetti et al., arXiv:2406.13352). We run four LLM models; three follow injections in baseline mode (gpt-oss-120b, Qwen2.5-72B, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, n=192 combined). Baseline Attack Success Rate (ASR): 19.3% [14.3%, 25.4%]; protected ASR: 0% (Wilson 95% CI upper bound 2.0%). Zero false positives across four benign task scenarios.

</details>


### [241] [Reversible Unlearnable Examples: Towards the Copyright Protection in Deep Learning Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06211)
*Binze Wang,Jinyu Tian,Xingrun Wang,Xiaochen Yuan,Jianqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"可逆不可学习示例"的综合版权保护机制，同时应对非法模型训练和恶意数据泄露两大威胁。通过最小化输入与输出的互信息生成扰动，使模型学习到图像的不相关特征以阻止非法训练；同时设计双提取器策略（两个不同的水印提取器）消除不可学习扰动对水印提取的负面影响。在ImageNet、CIFAR10和Pets数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习依赖大规模数据集，数据版权保护至关重要。现有不可学习示例方法忽视了数据泄露风险，而仅简单组合可用性攻击与水印技术会导致负面交互效应，无法同时解决非法模型训练和恶意数据泄露两大威胁。现有方法的组合无法有效应对这两个安全问题，亟需一个统一且互不干扰的版权保护机制。

Method: 1）针对非法训练：通过最小化模型输入与输出的互信息来生成不可学习扰动，诱导模型学习输入图像中的不相关特征，使扰动具有较强的泛化能力。2）针对数据泄露：设计双提取策略，使用两个不同的水印提取器，以消除不可学习扰动对水印提取的副作用影响，确保水印可被可靠提取以验证数据所有权。

Result: 在ImageNet、CIFAR10和Pets三个图像数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能够为图像提供全面的版权保护，既有效阻止未经授权的模型训练，又能可靠提取水印以应对数据泄露，同时避免了可用性攻击与水印技术简单组合时的负面交互效应。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种全新的综合版权保护机制，将不可学习扰动与水印提取进行协同设计，通过互信息最小化实现强泛化性的扰动生成，并通过双提取器策略解决扰动与水印之间的冲突，为深度学习中的数据版权提供了双重的、互不干扰的保护方案。

Abstract: Significant advancements in deep learning have been made possible by the utilization of large datasets, underscoring the critical importance of copyright protection. Adding meticulously designed perturbations to examples, making them unlearnable has become a crucial approach for safeguarding data copyright. Existing methods for creating unlearnable examples overlook the risk of data leakage, which can threaten data ownership. Thus, copyright protection in deep learning faces two main threats: illegal model training and malicious data leakage. We investigate that these two threats cannot be solved by straightforwardly combining existing availability attacks and watermarking techniques as their negative interaction effects. Therefore, in this paper, we propose a novel copyright protection mechanism for the aforementioned security concerns. Considering that the prevention of unauthorized model training requires powerful generalizability of unlearnable perturbations, we generate perturbations to induce the model to learn uncorrelated features of input images. It works by minimizing the mutual information of the input and output of the model. On the other hand, to eliminate the side impact of unlearnable perturbations on the watermark extraction, we design a dual extraction strategy by using two distinct watermark extractors. Extensive experiments on the image datasets {ImageNet, CIFAR10, and Pets} show that our proposed method could provide comprehensive copyright protection to images. The code is available at {https://github.com/Yeah21/ReversibleUnlearnableExamples}.

</details>


### [242] [Game Hopping in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06261)
*Stefan Dziembowski,Grzegorz Fabiański,Daniele Micciancio,Rafał Stefański*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍HOPSCOTCH，一个基于Lean 4的框架，用于机械化可计算健全的基于博弈的密码学证明，并通过四个示例验证了其有效性，其中包括首个非恒定深度的GGM伪随机函数机械化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学中的博弈跳跃（game-hopping）证明虽然广泛使用，但容易出现人为错误。现有的机械化证明框架要么依赖专门的证明系统，难以与通用数学库集成，要么对非恒定深度的安全证明支持不足。作者希望提供一个能够与Lean完整生态（包括Mathlib）深度集成、同时支持标准博弈跳跃范式的机械化证明框架。

Method: HOPSCOTCH使用浅嵌入（shallow embedding），将预言机和归约定义为普通的Lean定义，从而直接利用Lean生态和Mathlib中的数学理论（如有限群论）。博弈跳跃证明被表示为显式的形式化对象，其构造子对应博弈跳跃论证的标准步骤。框架通过一个通用计算健全性定理来解释这些证明对象，构造针对假设的归约并推导区分器优势的具体上界。预言机之间的观测等价通过状态抽象（state-abstraction）方法论建立，支持添加/遗忘状态、懒惰采样替换急切采样等变换。

Result: 框架通过四个案例验证：encrypt-then-MAC的IND-CCA安全、基于DDH假设的ElGamal加密安全性、一步保密到公钥IND-CPA安全的蕴含关系，以及GGM伪随机函数构造。其中GGM证明是首个针对非恒定深度的机械化证明。

Conclusion: HOPSCOTCH成功地将可计算健全的密码学博弈证明融入Lean 4生态，通过浅嵌入实现对通用数学理论的直接调用，并利用显式证明对象简化了博弈跳跃证明的构造、自动化与检查。该框架在多个经典密码学方案上得到验证，并首次实现了非恒定深度GGM构造的机械化证明。

Abstract: We present HOPSCOTCH, a Lean 4 framework for mechanizing computationally sound, game-based cryptographic proofs. Security definitions are expressed as indistinguishability between stateful probabilistic oracles, and proofs follow the standard game-hopping paradigm. HOPSCOTCH uses a shallow embedding: oracles and reductions are ordinary Lean definitions, enabling direct integration with the full Lean ecosystem, including general mathematical theories from Mathlib, such as finite-group theory. A game-hopping proof in HOPSCOTCH is represented as an explicit formal object whose constructors correspond to the standard steps of a game-hopping argument, making proofs easier to construct, automate, and inspect. We prove a general computational soundness theorem that interprets these proof objects by constructing reductions against the assumptions they use and deriving a concrete bound on the advantage of any distinguisher. Observational equivalence between oracles is established using a state-abstraction methodology: a simple yet powerful approach that supports transformations such as adding or forgetting state and replacing eager sampling with lazy sampling. We illustrate the framework with formalized proofs of the IND-CCA security of encrypt-then-MAC, the security of ElGamal encryption from DDH, the implication from one-time secrecy to public-key IND-CPA security, and the GGM pseudorandom-function construction. To the best of our knowledge, the last is the first mechanized proof of GGM for non-constant depth.

</details>


### [243] [A Sound Translation from Tamarin to ProVerif: Enabling Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06315)
*Kevin Morio,Yavor Ivanov,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从Tamarin到ProVerif的可靠翻译方法，支持对两种安全协议验证工具进行系统化对比，并通过121个Tamarin模型的评估验证了翻译的有效性和ProVerif的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tamarin和ProVerif是两种主流的协议形式化验证工具，但它们在底层形式体系和验证技术上存在显著差异（Tamarin使用多重集重写规则并提供可靠且完备的验证，ProVerif使用应用π演算扩展并可能产生不完备结果），这使得对两者的系统比较极为困难。作者希望通过提出可靠的翻译方法实现严谨的工具对比。

Method: 提出从Tamarin到ProVerif的可靠翻译，包含公式重写、多重集重写语义编码和同步事件处理等新技术。定义了忠实翻译子集，并给出了可靠性和完备性的形式化证明。对翻译应用到121个Tamarin模型进行评估。

Result: 翻译覆盖了562/566个引理任务。在非XOR且有确定性结果的任务中，247个中有246个结果一致（唯一不一致被明确标记为使用不完备模型）。在362个Tamarin返回布尔结果且ProVerif完成逻辑结果的任务中，ProVerif在334个（92.3%）任务上更快，中位每任务运行时间比为6.74倍，峰值内存比为6.24倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的从Tamarin到ProVerif的翻译在忠实子集内保证了可靠性（ProVerif验证通过的属性在Tamarin模型中同样成立）和完备性（不涉及攻击者知识的exists-trace属性被保留），并通过大规模实验验证了翻译的有效性以及ProVerif在效率和内存占用上的明显优势。XOR等尽力而为的编码不在这些保证范围内。

Abstract: Tamarin and ProVerif are two prominent tools for the formal verification of security protocols. They share the same high-level goal but differ significantly in their underlying formalisms and verification techniques, making a systematic comparison challenging: Tamarin uses multiset rewrite rules with sound and complete verification, whereas ProVerif employs an extension of the applied-pi calculus that provides fast but potentially incomplete results.
  We present a sound translation from Tamarin to ProVerif that enables a rigorous comparison of the two tools. It introduces novel techniques for formula rewriting, encoding multiset rewrite semantics, and handling simultaneous events, supporting an extensive subset of Tamarin's features, including multiset rewrite rules, lemmas, and restrictions, while precisely characterizing the cases where faithful translation is not possible. We provide formal proofs: within the faithful fragment, soundness ensures that any property verified in ProVerif also holds in the original Tamarin model, and completeness ensures that exists-trace properties not involving attacker knowledge are preserved. Best-effort encodings, in particular XOR, are reported separately and are outside these guarantees.
  Finally, we evaluate our translation on 121 Tamarin models. The translation covers 562 of 566 lemma tasks. Among non-XOR tasks with definitive results from both tools, 246 of 247 agree, with the remaining verdict explicitly flagged as using an incomplete model. Among the 362 tasks for which Tamarin returns a Boolean result and ProVerif completes with a logical result, ProVerif is faster in 334 cases (92.3%), with median per-task runtime and peak-memory ratios of 6.74x and 6.24x, respectively.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个不确定性量化的质量多样性（QD）框架，用于在24小时时间分配建议中平衡预期健康效益与模型置信度，从而生成更可靠的日常活动组合推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间使用优化方法仅最大化预期健康收益，忽略了数据驱动预测中的不确定性，可能导致不切实际的建议。健康决策中忽视不确定性会产生不可靠的时间使用推荐。

Method: 利用组成数据分析（compositional data analysis）从大型儿童队列数据集（n>1000）推导目标函数，将预测不确定性嵌入质量多样性（QD）优化过程，通过基于变量和基于目标的两种行为表征探索解空间。

Result: 框架揭示了多样化的高质量时间使用组合以及与健康结果相关的显式关系，同时将建议推向不确定性更低的区域而不牺牲高质量结构，实现更可靠的行为健康决策支持。

Conclusion: 将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，能够使时间使用建议在保持高质量结构的同时向低不确定性区域偏移，为行为健康领域提供更可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [245] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计期望性结局排序（DOOR）概率，以评估随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险。框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性函数，通过序贯风险集风险估计条件有序分布，并推导其高效影响函数。模拟显示TMLE-SL点估计性能最佳，CVTMLE-SL在区间推断上整体表现最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在获益-风险评估中，DOOR概率是一种直观且有临床意义的度量，但现有估计方法缺乏统一的协变量校正框架，且对不同估计器在有限样本下的表现缺乏系统比较，尤其是在复杂场景（如治疗效应异质性、重叠性不足）下的有效推断有待解决。

Method: 将DOOR概率表示为边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函；通过序贯风险集风险（sequential risk-set hazards）估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的高效影响函数（EIF）。模拟中比较G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW和TMLE，使用GLM或Super Learner（SL）作为nuisance估计器；进一步对AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL在交叉拟合（cross-fitting）下评估EIF推断性能，并采用抗菌药物耐药性领导小组（ARLG）多药耐药菌网络数据加以说明。

Result: 点估计模拟中，TMLE-SL表现最强且最稳定，AIPW-SL次之。EIF推断评估中，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、有序分布恢复、标准误精度和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最佳。

Conclusion: CVTMLE-SL是估计DOOR概率并构建可靠推断的推荐方法，该统一框架为临床试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评估提供了可行的因果推断解决方案。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [246] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在单调对抗者插入额外正确标注样本的模型中，学习器的期望误差率在VC维d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，在d=1时为Θ(1/n)，表明额外对数因子是缺乏可交换性的内在代价，而非特定算法的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人引入了一个新模型：单调对抗者在观察干净的i.i.d.标注样本后，可以插入有限数量的额外正确标注样本。由于插入依赖于干净样本，组合样本不再可交换。他们证明了经验风险最小化达到O((d/n)log(n/d))的期望误差，但所有已知最优学习器都无法达到PAC学习最优的Θ(d/n)速率。作者提出的核心问题是：这个额外对数因子是特定算法的产物，还是缺乏可交换性导致的内在代价？

Method: 本文通过构造精确的上下界来回答这个问题。上界方面，通过一个简单的非真学习器，其分析推广了单包含图（one-inclusion graph）的留一法论证，实现d=1时的Θ(1/n)速率。下界方面，通过一个统一的构造：显式构造一个类别和先验分布，使得两个在非平凡质量点上不同的目标假设产生相同样本，从而证明所有已知插入预算下，VC维d≥2时的下界为Ω((d/n)log(n/d))。同时将结果推广到Littlestone维。

Result: 在VC维d≥2时，极小极大期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))；在d=1时为Θ(1/n)。同样的速率在将d替换为Littlestone维d_L时也成立，因此干净的在线到批量速率O(d_L/n)同样不可达到。

Conclusion: 额外对数因子是缺乏可交换性的内在结果，而非特定算法的缺陷。令人意外的是，即使插入的样本都是正确标注的，添加这些样本反而会使学习在VC维d≥2时更困难（增加对数因子），即使对于在在线学习中具有有限错误界（Littlestone维有限）的类别也是如此。所有下界都来自一个单一的简单构造。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [247] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Deep Generalised Mixed Model（深度广义混合模型）的新型神经网络架构，将广义混合效应模型推广到深度学习领域，用于分析体验采样方法（ESM）收集的纵向数据。该模型结合固定效应和随机效应进行半参数化灵活建模，采用变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法进行估计，能够处理高维、多分布类型的纵向结果，并在数据缺失完全随机（MAR）时提供有效推断。模型应用于GrowIt!青少年情绪研究及多项模拟实验，结果显示出潜力但存在模型不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESM数据分析面临两大挑战：传统统计技术在应对高维数据时扩展性不足，而机器学习方法因数据缺失（如青少年因负面情绪而退出研究导致的选择性缺失）引入偏差。作者被GrowIt!应用中收集的青少年疫情期间日常情绪数据所驱动，旨在开发一种既能处理高维数据、又能对缺失数据（特别是MAR类型）提供有效推断的分析方法。

Method: 作者提出Deep Generalised Mixed Model架构，将广义混合效应模型推广至深度学习框架。该模型通过固定效应和随机效应半参数化地灵活建模数据的均值与相关结构。估计过程采用变分自编码器（VAE）的改编版本，并辅以贝叶斯数据增强算法，使模型能够适应各种分布类型的纵向结果，支持高维场景，并在数据缺失完全随机（missing-at-random）时提供有效推断。

Result: 将Deep Generalised Mixed Model应用于GrowIt!研究和多项模拟实验中，结果显示该模型具有良好的应用潜力，但同时也暴露出由于模型不稳定性（model instability）导致的次优性能。

Conclusion: Deep Generalised Mixed Model为ESM数据提供了一种结合深度学习灵活性与混合效应模型推断能力的创新方案，在理论上能够应对高维与缺失数据处理挑战，但当前版本的模型稳定性仍有待改善，需要进一步优化才能达到理想的分析效果。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [248] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使VARMA模型估计的计算成本与序列长度T无关的框架，通过偏自相关重参数化保证平稳性和可逆性，利用Parseval恒等式将损失函数转化为仅依赖固定大小充分统计量的形式，从而实现了在较高维度下（d=10到d=40）可行且优于VAR的估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型在中等维度以上被认为不实用，原因是似然函数非凸、参数化仅等价类可识别且每次评估需遍历整个序列。然而其移动平均项能以少量参数匹配纯自回归需要许多滞后才能捕捉的动态。这促使作者寻找消除这一计算障碍的估计框架。

Method: 框架包含三个关键要素：(1)偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2)对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角与对角外元素分别设置尺度；(3)通过Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式，使损失函数仅依赖于固定大小的充分统计量，以截断长度的近线性成本评估。由此产生两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计器。该机制还可扩展到季节性动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 两种估计器都能以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。经验上，在d=10到d=40的维度范围内（经典条件MLE产生非可逆拟合且预测发散），估计器保持接近oracle预测误差，并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量式ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架使基于似然的VARMA估计以每次迭代成本独立于序列长度T的方式，达到从业者此前只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，打破了VARMA在中等维度上的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


### [249] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概率回归树（PRTrees）框架，提出三种在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量值的策略（均匀概率法、部分观测法和降维平滑法），无需先验插补，并在多个真实数据集上验证了其在处理高缺失率数据时优于经典CART的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树面对缺失值时需要预先插补，而概率回归树（PRTrees）作为一个平滑且一致的回归树替代方案，尚未能直接在树构建过程中处理缺失预测变量。本文旨在消除对先验插补的依赖，将PRTree框架扩展为能够原生处理任意模式缺失协变量值的方法。

Method: 提出三种缺失值处理策略：(1) 均匀概率法，将缺失值以均匀概率分配到各分裂分支；(2) 部分观测法，仅利用可观测部分的信息进行概率分配；(3) 降维平滑法，通过降低维度进行平滑处理。这些修改被设计为保证概率守恒和边际相容性等原有概率特性，且适用于任意缺失模式。

Result: 在多个具有不同缺失程度的真实数据集上与经典回归树对比，结果表明缺失值的处理方式（fill策略）对预测性能的影响往往大于平滑分布或代理变量选择准则。在缺失比例较高的数据集中，所提方法经常优于CART，同时保持了树模型的解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 概率树构建的有效性强烈依赖于缺失观测的处理方式。本文提出的三种策略为PRTree框架提供了原生处理缺失值的能力，在数据高缺失率场景下展现出优于经典方法的预测性能，并保持了树模型的可解释性优势。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）提供了联合控制条件有效性与Oracle效率的有限样本保证，在Hölder正则性等假设下给出条件覆盖缺口和长度误差的高概率界，并分析了数据分割学习得分下的扩展结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测具有有限样本分布自由的边际覆盖性质，但边际有效可能掩盖严重的协变量特定误校准，而精确的分布自由条件覆盖在有限样本下不可达。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缩小这一差距，但现有理论缺乏对实际局部化集合的有限样本保证，无法同时控制条件有效性和Oracle效率。

Method: 在条件得分CDF满足Hölder正则性及标准密度和核假设下，对固定的得分函数，证明在实现局部化邻域上一致成立的条件覆盖缺口和相对Oracle的长度误差的高概率界。界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差项和随校准规模递减的校准项。对于数据分割学习得分（如共形分位数回归中的枢轴得分），将均匀局部保证分解为固定得分校准误差和均匀得分估计误差。

Result: 推导出RLCP的条件覆盖缺口和长度误差的统一高概率界，明确展示了带宽bias-variance权衡，阐明了RLCP何时能追踪Oracle。当得分针对枢轴得分学习时，改进的学习能锐化局部化保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次为RLCP的实际局部化集合提供了同时控制条件有效性和Oracle效率的有限样本理论保证，深化了对RLCP带宽权衡及学习得分影响的理解。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [251] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中标签翻转噪声下，早期停止的梯度下降（GD）能够克服最大间隔插值分类器的统计次优性，在特定高斯混合模型下达到极小极大最优的0-1风险。核心贡献包括：将逻辑损失风险转化为0-1风险的新校准结果（消除标准界限中的平方根率），以及证明插值分类器可能需要比早期停止指数级更多的样本来达到相同的超额风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化分类中，训练数据即使底层分布不可分也可能线性可分，此时逻辑损失的梯度下降在方向上收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，但其隐式偏差在统计上可能次优。作者希望通过早期停止来克服这种次优性，实现统计最优的性能。

Method: （1）在具有标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，分析梯度下降在逻辑损失上迭代早期停止的统计性质，给出尖锐的上界；（2）建立匹配的统计下界，覆盖任意分类器；（3）提出新的校准结果，将超额逻辑风险转化为超额0-1风险，处理标签翻转噪声引起的模型错误设定，并去除标准界限中的平方根率；（4）建立线性插值器的下界，与早期停止对比。

Result: （1）在协方差谱快速且连续衰减（包括多项式和指数衰减）的情况下，早期停止的GD在适当的预言机停止时间达到极小极大最优的超额0-1风险；（2）新的校准结果消除了标准转换中的平方根率；（3）实验验证了理论速率；（4）证明线性插值器可能需要指数级更多的样本才能达到与早期停止相同的超额风险。

Conclusion: 早期停止是一种有效克服过参数化分类中插值分类器统计次优性的策略，能够在标签翻转噪声下实现极小极大最优性能，而插值方法在样本效率上存在根本性的劣势。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [252] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于持久性图（PD）空间上随机动力学的强化学习框架，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义受控马尔可夫过程，并证明其马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性与几何遍历性，从而在PD空间上建立唯一平稳概率分布。框架通过平衡任务相关分布目标、图谱保真度和复杂度缩减的奖励函数，实现自适应拓扑简化与概率建模，实验在合成及神经影像PD上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图虽在拓扑数据分析中被广泛使用，但现有文献大多将其视为静态对象，缺乏在PD空间上进行概率建模和随机演化的系统框架，难以支持动态演化、分布建模和目标导向的拓扑结构生成等任务。

Method: 1) 设计拓扑感知的局部编辑操作（如点的添加、删除、移动等）定义PD空间上的受控马尔可夫过程；2) 建立马尔可夫链不可约、非周期、几何遍历的条件，保证唯一平稳分布存在；3) 构造多目标奖励函数，涵盖分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计量以及结构保持压缩；4) 采用强化学习优化动力学策略，实现自适应拓扑简化与概率建模。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像持久性图上，所提框架能够在降低图复杂度的同时保持主导拓扑结构，验证了其在自适应拓扑简化和概率建模方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为PD空间的随机动力学提供了理论基础和实用算法框架，弥合了静态拓扑分析和动态概率模型之间的鸿沟，开启了持久性图强化学习与生成式建模的新方向。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Characterizations of independence between order statistics and rank indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05428)
*Roberto Vila,Frederico Almeida*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了独立非负随机变量中顺序统计量与其对应秩指示量之间的独立性，通过一种基于条件概率重新加权概率测度的框架建立了通用刻画，并给出了相关权重函数、条件分布的显式表达式以及基于Kolmogorov和Wasserstein距离的分布无关的独立性偏离度量。特别关注最小和最大顺序统计量，得到了单重和多重删失机制下独立右删失和左删失的新刻画，并基于比例风险和比例逆风险模型建立了充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序统计量及其秩指示量之间的独立性问题在统计学中具有基础性意义，尤其在删失数据分析中，秩指示量代表着观察值的相对位置，而顺序统计量本身是数据排序后的值。此前对这一独立性的刻画缺乏一般性框架，且现有方法往往局限于特定分布假设或特定删失机制，亟需一个统一的理论框架来理解这种独立性结构及其与删失机制的关系。

Method: 论文提出了一种通用框架：通过根据单个观测占据规定秩的条件概率重新加权其边际分布，构造一个新的概率测度来刻画独立秩与顺序统计量之间的独立性。在此框架下推导了权重函数和条件分布的显式表达式，并利用Kolmogorov距离和Wasserstein距离构造了分布无关的独立性偏离度量。针对最小和最大顺序统计量进行了专门分析，在单重和多重删失情形下建立了新的独立性刻画，并通过比例风险和比例逆风险模型给出了充分条件。

Result: 得到了顺序统计量与秩指示量独立性的完全刻画，给出了权重函数和条件分布的封闭形式表达式；建立了基于比例风险和比例逆风险模型的充分条件，在经典单删失情形下推导了完整的刻画结果。通过离散和连续示例以及数值模拟验证了方法的适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个统一的概率测度框架来处理顺序统计量与秩指示量之间的独立性问题，不仅深化了对这一基础结构的理论理解，还为独立右删失和左删失（单重及多重删失机制）提供了新的刻画工具，具有理论和应用层面的广泛价值。

Abstract: We study the independence between an order statistic and its corresponding rank indicator for independent nonnegative random variables. A general characterization is established through a probability measure obtained by reweighting the distribution of a single observation according to its conditional probability of occupying a prescribed rank. This framework yields explicit expressions for the associated weighting functions and conditional distributions, as well as distribution-free measures of departure from independence based on Kolmogorov and Wasserstein distances. Special attention is devoted to the minimum and maximum order statistics, leading to new characterizations of independent right- and left-censoring under both single and multiple censoring mechanisms. We also establish sufficient conditions based on proportional hazards and proportional reversed hazards models and derive complete characterizations in the classical single-censoring setting. Discrete and continuous examples, together with numerical illustrations, are presented to demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [254] [Asset-specific limit order microstructure noise: Parameter estimation and empirical evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05838)
*Markus Bibinger,Adrian Grüber,Moritz Jirak*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将限价订单簿高频报价的单边微观结构噪声模型推广至捕获资产特定的噪声尾部行为，通过Gamma噪声分布假设，基于高频收益矩估计噪声尾部参数，并建立了1-依赖观测的中心极限定理，实证分析表明资产特定的噪声尾部参数在实践中具有相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单边微观结构噪声模型假设噪声尾部行为对所有资产相同，无法捕获资产特定的噪声尾部特征。噪声尾部参数对有效价格及其波动率的估计具有重要影响，因此需要一种能够估计资产特定噪声尾部参数的方法。

Method: 将单边微观结构噪声模型推广以捕获资产特定的噪声尾部行为；在指定参数化噪声模型（Gamma噪声分布）下，基于高频收益的矩来估计噪声尾部参数；为Gamma差分分布的1-依赖观测建立中心极限定理，并证明该定理在具有一般半鞅对数价格动态的卷积模型中同样成立。

Result: 建立了噪声尾部参数估计的中心极限定理，证明了其在一般半鞅对数价格动态的卷积模型中的有效性；基于近期NASDAQ限价订单簿数据的实证分析表明，资产特定的噪声尾部参数在实践中确实相关且具有显著差异。

Conclusion: 噪声尾部参数对有效价格和波动率的估计具有重要影响，资产特定的噪声尾部建模是必要的。所提出的矩估计方法和中心极限定理为实践中的噪声尾部参数估计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: The one-sided microstructure noise model for high-frequency quotes from a limit order book is generalized to capture asset-specific noise tail behaviour. Estimation of a noise tail parameter becomes the key step for inference. This is possible based on moments of the high-frequency returns when specifying a parametric noise model. For a Gamma noise distribution, we establish a central limit theorem for 1-dependent observations from the resulting Gamma difference distribution and prove that it is valid also in the convolution model with general semimartingale log-price dynamics. We highlight that the noise tail parameter has important implications for estimating the efficient price and its volatility. An empirical analysis of recent NASDAQ limit order book data demonstrates that asset-specific noise tail parameters are relevant in practice.

</details>


### [255] [Non-Gaussian fluctuations for traces of squared sample correlation matrices in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05871)
*Johannes Heiny,Xuechun Hu,Felix Seo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了由n个独立同分布p维随机向量构成的样本相关矩阵R的平方迹tr(R²)的极限理论。在有限四阶矩和p、n同比例增长的条件下，已知tr(R²)满足中心极限定理(CLT)，且其中心化和缩放序列是普适的（不依赖于条目分布）。本文证明：在对称性和正则变化假设（指数α）下，对任意维度增长率，当α>3时普适CLT仍然成立；当α<3时，存在一个临界维度增长率，在该点tr(R²)的波动变为非高斯；当维度p增长更快且α≤3时，建立了一个非普适的CLT，其归一化序列依赖于α的值。文中通过模拟研究验证了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献表明，在有限四阶矩且p与n成比例增长时，样本相关矩阵平方的迹满足普适的中心极限定理。然而，当数据来自重尾分布（正则变化假设）时，条目分布的特性可能导致CLT的普适性失效。本文旨在探讨在更一般（重尾）的条件下，普适CLT在何种参数范围内仍然成立，以及在何时失效并需要非普适的归一化。

Method: 本文采用对称性和正则变化（指数α）的假设来刻画重尾行为。通过允许维度p以任意速率相对于n增长，系统地分析tr(R²)的极限分布。对于α>3的情况，证明普适CLT仍然成立；对于α≤3的情况，识别出临界维度增长速率，并利用正则变化指数的具体值构造不同的归一化序列，从而建立非普适的CLT。最后通过数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: (1) 当α>3时，对所有维度增长率，普适CLT保持有效；(2) 当α<3时，存在一个临界维度增长率，在此处tr(R²)的波动呈现非高斯极限；3) 当p增长更快且α≤3时，得到了依赖于α的非普适CLT，且归一化序列随α值变化。模拟研究展示了理论与数值的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了样本相关矩阵平方迹的极限行为对尾部指数α的强依赖性：轻尾程度足以支撑四阶矩时普适性保持；而重尾（α≤3）情况下，需要根据维度增长速度和α的具体值调整归一化序列，极限分布从高斯变为非高斯。这为高维重尾数据下的随机矩阵理论提供了更完整的图景。

Abstract: We provide limit theory for the trace of the squared sample correlation matrix $\mathbf R$, constructed from $n$ observations of a $p$-dimensional random vector with iid components. If the entries have finite fourth moment and $p$ and $n$ grow proportionally, it is known that $\operatorname{tr}({\mathbf R}^2)$ satisfies a central limit theorem (CLT) and the centering and scaling sequences are universal in the sense that they do not depend on the entry distribution. Under symmetry and regular variation assumption with index $α$ and any growth rate of the dimension, we prove that the universal CLT remains valid for $α>3$. For $α<3$, we identify a critical dimension growth at which the fluctuations of $\operatorname{tr}({\mathbf R}^2)$ become non-Gaussian. Moreover, if the dimension $p$ grows faster and $α\le 3$ we establish a non-universal CLT with norming sequences depending on the value of $α$. Our findings are illustrated in a simulation study.

</details>


### [256] [Polynomial regression under a mixture of classical and Berkson errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05912)
*Oleksandr Liubimov,Alexander Kukush*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个多项式结构回归模型，其中协变量同时带有经典测量误差和Berkson测量误差的混合。在假设两类误差的方差及部分高阶矩已知、且不依赖正态性假设的条件下，使用校正得分（Corrected Score）方法构造了模型参数的一致估计量，并给出了渐近正态性的条件。在温和条件下，发现了渐近独立的估计量对，并通过模拟研究验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的数据往往同时受到经典测量误差和Berkson测量误差的混合影响，而以往的研究通常只考虑其中一种误差类型。此外，许多现有方法依赖于正态性假设，这在实际应用中可能不成立。因此需要一种不依赖正态性假设、能够同时处理两种测量误差混合情形的参数估计方法。

Method: 采用校正得分（Corrected Score）方法构造模型参数的一致估计量。该方法通过修正得分函数以消除测量误差带来的偏差。假定经典误差和Berkson误差的方差以及部分高阶矩已知，但不要求误差服从正态分布。进一步推导了估计量的渐近正态性条件，并研究了估计量对之间的渐近独立性。

Result: 构建了参数的一致估计量并给出了渐近正态性的充分条件；在温和条件下识别出了若干对渐近独立的估计量。模拟研究验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为同时存在经典与Berkson混合测量误差的多项式结构回归模型提供了一套不依赖正态性假设的估计框架，丰富了测量误差模型的理论与方法，对实际应用具有参考价值。

Abstract: A polynomial structural regression model is studied, where the covariate is observed with a mixture of the classical and Berkson measurement errors. Both variances of the classical and Berkson errors, as well as some of their higher moments are assumed to be known. Without normality assumptions, consistent estimators of model parameters are constructed using the Corrected Score method, and conditions for their asymptotic normality are given. Under mild conditions, we found pairs of asymptotically independent estimators. A simulation study illustrates the results.

</details>


### [257] [Bartlett Couplings of the Onion and Vine LKJ Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06116)
*Peter Reinhard Hansen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明扩展洋葱（extended-onion）和C-vine采样方法均源自更简单的行归一化Bartlett构造（基于Wang, Wu和Chu 2018年的受限Wishart表示），并建立了两种精确的行耦合。通过Gamma比率核算，归一化Bartlett、洋葱和传统对称Beta C-vine分别需要n-1、2(n-1)和n(n-1)次Gamma等价调用，且Bartlett方法在计算效率和数值精度上均优于两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LKJ分布是相关矩阵采样的标准工具，但现有的洋葱构造和C-vine构造存在计算开销大、需要重新生成随机量等问题。作者希望找到一个更简单、更高效的统一构造方法，以减少随机数调用次数并提高数值稳定性。

Method: 作者基于Wang、Wu和Chu（2018）提出的受限Wishart表示，采用行归一化Bartlett构造作为统一框架。通过两种精确的行耦合证明：一是洋葱方向向量中高斯向量的平方范数精确服从Gamma定律，满足Beta半径所需分量；二是该向量配合一个卡方变量可生成整行相互独立的C-vine偏相关系数及其对称Beta分布。在基准测试中，作者对三种方法的Gamma等价调用次数进行了核算和对比。

Result: 归一化Bartlett、洋葱和C-vine的Gamma等价调用次数分别为n-1、2(n-1)和n(n-1)。基准测试显示：在低维情况下Bartlett优于洋葱实现，并且在所有维度上持续优于C-vine实现。此外，Bartlett方法避免了减法补余运算，将小η时的零对角阈值从机器精度量级推移到次正规数（subnormal）范围。

Conclusion: 行归一化Bartlett构造是LKJ采样的统一且优越的方法：它比洋葱和C-vine更高效（更少的随机数调用）、数值上更稳定（避免减法相消），并且对任意实数η>0有效，仅需标准正态和卡方随机变量即可实现。

Abstract: The extended-onion and C-vine constructions of Lewandowski, Kurowicka and Joe (2009) are standard methods for sampling from the $\mathrm{LKJ}_n(η)$ distribution on correlation matrices. We show that both arise from the simpler row-normalized Bartlett construction associated with the restricted-Wishart representation of Wang, Wu and Chu (2018), which reuses random quantities that the classical samplers regenerate. Two exact row-wise couplings establish this: the squared norm of the Gaussian vector supplying the onion's direction has exactly the Gamma law required for one component of the Beta radius, and the same vector, with one chi-squared variate, generates the entire row of mutually independent C-vine partial correlations with their required symmetric-Beta laws. Under gamma-ratio accounting, normalized Bartlett, the onion, and the conventional symmetric-Beta C-vine require $n-1$, $2(n-1)$, and $n(n-1)$ Gamma-equivalent calls. Controlled benchmarks confirm a low-dimensional advantage over the onion implementation and a persistent advantage over the C-vine implementations examined; direct Bartlett normalization also avoids subtractive complements, moving the small-$η$ zero-diagonal threshold from machine-epsilon scale toward the subnormal range. The sampler is valid for every real $η>0$ and requires only standard normal and chi-squared variates.

</details>


### [258] [A Profile-Separation Framework for Quantitative Convergence of No-U-Turn Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06336)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了强对数凹目标分布下的多项式选择和有偏渐进式No-U-Turn采样器（NUTS），引入"轮廓分离"条件并综合诊断集中性、跳跃忠实度和整轨道能量控制，证明了高概率认证事件下每次倍增实现都能通过真正的U型转弯达到共同终端深度，并给出了无条件的转移界和确定性/期望形式梯度工作量界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强对数凹目标（满足mI_d≤∇²U(x)≤LI_d且Hessian Lipschitz连续）下的NUTS算法提供严格的理论收敛性分析，此前NUTS在一般条件下的混合速率和转移界缺乏严格保证。作者希望在不进行lazify操作的前提下，给出无条件（而非依赖特定事件）的转移复杂度上界。

Method: 引入"轮廓分离"（profile separation）这一关于平稳均值U型转弯诊断的充分符号条件；将其与诊断浓度（diagnostic concentration）、跳跃忠实度（leapfrog fidelity）和整轨道能量控制（whole-orbit energy control）结合，在高概率认证事件上证明每次倍增实现经真正U型转弯达到公共终端深度。通过终端深度传递论证（terminal-depth transfer argument）推导受限电导和热启动混合；对多项式选择与有偏渐进式选择分别给出转移界，并处理最大深度上限对梯度工作的影响（均等时确定性界，否则依赖上限的期望和高概率形式）。

Result: 多项式选择的转移界为Õ(1+a_★²κ²(1+γ)^{4/3})，有偏渐进式选择为Õ(1+a_★⁴κ³(1+γ)²)，均为无条件界；梯度工作在工作深度上限与认证深度相当时为确定性界，否则取上限感知的期望和高概率形式。框架恢复了高斯维度依赖，对非线性乘积目标提供总体轮廓和精确诊断验证，给出近各向同性的实用树证书特化，并量化固定后热启动度量如何去除线性各向异性。

Conclusion: 通过引入轮廓分离条件，NUTS在强对数凹目标下的混合复杂度可以获得无条件、非lazified的严格转移界，且梯度工作量在合理深度上限下是确定性的。该框架同时适用于两种选择机制，覆盖高斯情形并推广到非线性乘积目标，为NUTS的理论保证提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: We study multinomial and biased-progressive No-U-Turn Samplers for strongly log-concave targets satisfying $mI_d\preceq \nabla^2U(x)\preceq LI_d,$ and $\|\nabla^2U(x)-\nabla^2U(y)\|_{\mathrm F}\le γL^{3/2}\|x-y\|$ with \(κ\coloneqq L/m\). We introduce profile separation, a sufficient sign condition on the stationary mean U-turn diagnostics, and combine it with diagnostic concentration, leapfrog fidelity, and whole-orbit energy control to show that on a high-probability certification event, every doubling realization reaches a common terminal depth through a genuine U-turn. If \(T_\star\) is the selected physical trajectory length and \(a_\star=\sqrt m\,T_\star\), a terminal-depth transfer argument yields restricted conductance and warm-start mixing without lazifying either kernel. Up to logarithmic warm-start and accuracy factors, the transition bounds are \[
  \widetilde O\!\left(
  1+a_\star^2κ^2(1+γ)^{4/3}
  \right)
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  \widetilde O\!\left(
  1+a_\star^4κ^3(1+γ)^2
  \right) \] for multinomial and biased-progressive selection, respectively. These transition bounds are unconditional. Gradient-work bounds are deterministic when the maximum-depth cap is comparable to the certified depth and otherwise take cap-aware expected and high-probability forms. The framework recovers the Gaussian dimension dependence under these work-accounting conditions, provides population-profile and exact-diagnostic verification for nonlinear product targets, a near-isotropic specialization of the practical-tree certificate, and quantifies when a fixed post-warmup metric removes linear anisotropy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的、已完整构建并通过集成测试的税务感知型投资组合推荐应用，采用FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，将自然语言投资目标路由到六个委托之一，并通过"自监督跨资产编码器+MoE分配策略+LoRA适配器"的三阶段强化学习系统生成实时、券商集成的投资建议。系统已在Alpaca模拟交易环境完成端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放，属于部署前新兴应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者无法获得机构客户享有的个性化、税务感知型投资组合管理服务。现有机器人投顾依赖静态规则，机构级系统又需要账户门槛和复杂技术栈，普通个人投资者无法接触。本文旨在填补这一差距，提供功能完整的应用以服务散户。

Method: 构建FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，用户用自然语言描述投资目标，路由到六个投资委托之一。采用三阶段强化学习系统：(1) 自监督跨资产编码器；(2) 带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts(MoE)分配策略；(3) 轻量级LoRA适配器，基于个人券商标记行为个性化推荐而不重训共享模型。系统包含多用户认证、预览后确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作完整链。开发方法论上强调端到端经验验证优于信任检查点元数据。

Result: 系统功能完整，已针对Alpaca券商API（模拟交易模式）完成端到端集成测试，包括多用户认证和完整信任流程。进行了14天滚动前推回测（含自助法置信区间）作为部署前初步验证。同时报告了若干实用工程教训：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据的价值。

Conclusion: 该系统是部署前阶段的完整应用，具有明确的完全部署路径。虽然当前验证结果仅为初步的部署前验证而非生产性能，但作者诚实地报告了这一状态。论文中的工程经验被认为可推广至其他基于外部实时数据源构建的应用强化学习系统。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [260] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究模型评估中自适应查询（根据先前响应选择后续测试）与非自适应查询（预先固定所有测试）之间的理论差异。在有限结果空间上，作者证明了当且仅当两个分布类在成对条件概率上具有正分离度时可学习的条件；并构造了一个随机非自适应程序，用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个预先固定的查询即可模拟任意T查询自适应策略的转录。最终证明最坏情况下的固定误差自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)，表明交互最多带来二次方缩减，而非指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中，测试者可以选择自适应地根据之前的响应调整后续测试，也可以预先固定所有测试。理解这两种模式之间的能力差异对于评估协议的设计至关重要，尤其是确定交互是否带来实质性的查询复杂度优势。

Method: 在条件查询模型（conditional-query model）框架下，作者分析有限结果空间上的分布类区分问题。首先刻画可区分性条件（成对条件概率的分离度），然后构造随机化非自适应程序来模拟自适应测试者的转录，通过总变差距离保证模拟精度，并提供匹配的下界构造以刻画自适应差距。

Result: 证明了：(1) 当真可实现区分性时，两类的成对条件概率分离度严格为正；当分离度为零时，最优最坏误差恰为1/2且不随查询预算改善。(2) 任意T查询自适应策略可用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个预选查询的随机非自适应程序模拟，转录总变差距离≤ρ。(3) 构造了常数量级自适应查询复杂度但Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度的匹配族，最坏情况固定误差自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互式评估至多将所需测试数量降低一个二次方因子（从N²降至常数），但并不能产生指数级的查询优势。这为模型评估协议的设计提供了理论边界：自适应查询有价值但有限，非自适应查询在最优模拟下也可实现相当的能力。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [261] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限VC维d≥1的假设类H⊆{-1,+1}^X，构造了一个学习器，实现了统计上最优的风险界：通过n个i.i.d.样本，对任意0<δ≤1/2，以至少1-δ的概率保证L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7×10⁸·(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)。这解决了agnostic PAC学习在固定L*下的样本复杂度问题（直至通用常数），匹配了Devroye、Györfi和Lugosi的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agnostic PAC学习框架下，给定有限VC维假设类的统计最优风险界长期以来存在理论与实践之间的间隙。虽然已知下界表明风险应随√(L*/n)和d/n尺度增长，但此前构造的算法是否能同时达到这一最优率尚未完全确定。本文旨在构造一个能够同时达到这两个项的学习器，从而在固定L*层面彻底解决样本复杂度问题。

Method: 本文构造了一个新的学习算法，在有限VC维假设类上实现了统一的风险界。该算法通过精细地处理经验风险与真实风险之间的偏差，实现了依赖L*的风险界中√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n)和(d+log(1/δ))/n两项的同时最优。具体构造方式结合了概率集中不等式与VC维的结构性质，利用了比经典uniform convergence更精细的局部化分析技术。

Result: 作者构造的学习器达到了风险界L(ĥ) ≤ L* + c·(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，其中c=7×10⁸为通用常数。该界在固定L*下匹配了Devroye、Györfi和Lugosi在《A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition》中给出的下界，确认了其最优性（达到通用常数级别）。

Conclusion: 本文证明了对于任意有限VC维假设类，agnostic PAC学习在固定L*下存在达到统计最优风险界的学习器，样本复杂度问题在通用常数的精度下获得了最终解答。这一结果填补了理论上界与下界之间的空白，对统计学习理论中风险界的研究具有奠基性意义。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


### [262] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlowAdam混合优化器，结合Adam与连续梯度流ODE积分，并通过Soft Momentum Injection平滑模式切换中的动量融合，在耦合参数优化任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法采用对角坐标级预条件，依赖坐标系统且无法有效处理密集或旋转的参数耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络），因为每个参数被独立处理，导致在这些场景下性能受限。

Method: FlowAdam在Adam基础上引入通过ODE实现的连续梯度流积分。当EMA统计量检测到优化景观困难时，切换至裁剪的ODE积分。核心创新是Soft Momentum Injection，在模式转换期间将ODE速度与Adam动量进行平滑混合，避免朴素混合方法导致的训练崩溃。

Result: 在耦合优化基准上，ODE积分提供隐式正则化，将低秩矩阵/张量恢复的留出误差降低10-22%，Jester真实协作过滤降低6%，超越调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良好条件化任务（CIFAR-10）上与Adam持平；MovieLens-100K证明收益来自耦合参数交互而非偏差估计；消融实验表明soft injection必不可少，硬替换使准确率从100%降至82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过自适应混合Adam与梯度流ODE积分，并借助Soft Momentum Injection实现平滑过渡，有效解决了Adam在耦合参数场景下的局限，同时保持良好条件化任务上的性能。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [263] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Adam与梯度下降在低秩隐式偏差上的本质差异源于优化器的规范等变性（gauge equivariance）。梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足该性质，而Adam、RMSProp等坐标逐点方法不满足。通过结构定理和迁移定理，作者用矩阵感知、Transformer和超光谱数据实验系统性验证了各向异性预条件破坏了低秩恢复偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在分解模型W=UV^T上对低秩解有隐式偏差，但Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备此性质。作者希望追溯这一差异的根本原因，理解何种优化器属性决定了低秩恢复能力，从而为优化器设计提供理论指导。

Method: 首先以损失的规范对称性(U,V)↦(UQ,VQ)为出发点，证明梯度流低秩机制仅对规范等变优化器有效。结构定理刻画了无记忆等变规则恰好是Gram决定的左预条件算子；迁移定理将梯度流的路径属性推广到公共标量流。随后在欠定矩阵感知、Transformer和超光谱数据集上对九种更新规则进行系统排序和验证，并引入"谱调度"统一Muon的相反对立实验结果。

Result: 坐标逐点到共享标量预条件的一参数族单调恢复偏差，证实各向异性是导致Adam等优化器失去低秩偏差的原因。谱调度表明等速率更新能精确恢复低秩目标但在谱尾增长时失去优势。在Transformer中，Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价的初始化，最终W_Q^TW_K的相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在超光谱数据上，梯度下降在匹配训练损失的情况下将留出误差降低43-44%。

Conclusion: 基选择不是调参细节，而是关于优化器选择哪个插值的决策。规范等变性是低秩恢复偏差转移的必要条件（非充分条件），各向异性预条件是Adam类优化器失去低秩偏差的根源。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [264] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中仅将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定的高斯先验，并不能保证风格表示与类别信息解耦。作者推导出完全分解，证明此条件是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一，消除它必要但不充分。实验表明，在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平），四种缓解策略只能将探针准确率降至21%-46%。后验条件先验和风格库方法在外部评估中部分缓解了问题。核心结论：仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度无法证明与类别标签的独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型通常通过将风格潜变量的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验来正则化，并以此作为风格表示与类别信息独立的证据。作者质疑这一普遍假设的正确性，试图从理论推导和实证两方面揭示边缘分布匹配的局限性。

Method: 作者首先进行理论推导，发展出一个精确分解，表明边缘分布匹配仅是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一。然后使用案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线进行广泛的实证分析，包括线性探针、全局MMD评估、聚类准确率、外部类别条件生成评估，以及六种独立扰动测试（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督方式）和四种缓解策略的测试。

Result: 案例模型和四个基线在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率恢复类别标签。模型达到99.15%聚类准确率，但外部评估的类别条件生成仅成功16%。六种扰动下泄露依然存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97外部类别生成分数，但在CIFAR-10上仅0.41；风格库方法在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布计算的任何散度都无法证明其与类别标签的独立性；报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的性质。边缘匹配是必要条件而非充分条件，需要额外的约束或更严格的条件才能实现真正的因子化解耦。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [265] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）输出头，将预测中的不确定性分解为三种可解释的源头（离散模式选择、有界系统漂移、随机噪声），并分别用二进制、有界和随机生成器表示，支持闭式似然、跨步骤代数耦合推理及分析性风险度量与多模态共形预测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头（高斯混合或单一共形区域）无法分离真实预测任务中存在的不同来源的不确定性，包括离散模态选择、所选模态内的有界系统漂移以及不可约的随机噪声，亟需一种能明确区分并分别建模这些不确定性来源的方法。

Method: HProbZ将三种不确定性分别编码为zonotope的二进制生成器（离散模式选择）、有界生成器（系统漂移）和随机生成器（噪声），通过卷积获得闭式似然。跨预测步共享有界生成器实现代数耦合，使观测一步即可通过单次前向传播精化所有剩余步的预测分布。理论上证明三种生成器可从似然中识别（至置换），且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表示上不同；推理时可获得解析的逐模式风险和无分布的多模态共形集。

Result: 在代表性预测基准上的实验表明，HProbZ相对于同编码器混合基线具有有效性，同时提供了高斯混合或凸共形预测器无法共同提供的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ通过将三种不确定性来源显式分离并由共享有界生成器耦合预测步，在保持闭式可计算性的同时提供了可辨识性与表示独特性，为具有多模态、有界漂移和噪声分布特性的预测任务提供了一种更具结构和解释力的概率输出方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [266] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，一种直接基于次梯度操作的Langevin扩散离散化方法，用于从非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的势函数对应的目标分布中采样。算法采用taming技术处理超线性梯度，并给出Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，常数以维度和逆温度显式追踪，改进了现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法的收敛速率。此外还提供了相关的优化问题超额风险估计，并在GPT-2谱系LLM的正则化预训练势上验证了假设，其坐标提升变体在预训练任务上可与调优后的AdamW和Muon竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Langevin采样算法在处理同时具备非光滑性、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布时存在不足：次梯度基算法收敛速率有限，而光滑化预处理计算成本高昂。作者旨在设计一个无需光滑化、能直接处理次梯度且稳定收敛的算法，同时提供显式常数以保证实际可用的非渐近保证，并为大型模型（如LLM）预训练中的非凸非光滑优化问题提供理论支撑。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)，其核心创新包括：(1) 直接对次梯度进行操作，避免昂贵的函数光滑化步骤；(2) 引入taming技术（对梯度项进行规范化缩放）以应对超线性梯度增长导致的数值不稳定，生成显式稳定格式；(3) 对非光滑非凸目标推导Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数显式依赖维度和逆温度；(4) 进一步给出优化问题的超额风险界，并针对GPT-2类LLM的正则化预训练势验证假设的显式常数；(5) 提出坐标提升变体（boosted coordinate-wise SG-TULA）用于实际预训练。
